## Bangalore

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble prostrated 108 obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to your lotus feet, which are always surrounded by hundreds and thousands of devotees.

You are the ocean of causeless mercy, the pastime of transcendental ecstasy. You came to this world upon your own will, just to carry on the mission of the supreme will. No one can be more fortunate than ourselves because of being initiated by your divine self. Although we are lower than the worm in the stool, out of your magnanimity and divine potency you have given us the process of purification by pouring upon us the nectar of Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* in the form of devotional service. How rare the personalities like you are! They come and go just like the lightning in the sky. O dear beloved Gurudeva Śrīla Prabhupāda, we offer you our unlimited obeisances because you have established love of Godhead within this present world, where people are simply interested only in sense gratification. Being fully absorbed in gross sense gratification, they have missed your nectarlike presence. How unfortunate they are. Therefore, our only prayer is that you please give us enough strength to save these fallen conditioned souls. Without your mercy, they cannot be relieved from the clutches of *māyā.* We therefore hope that you will permit us to take shelter of a dust particle from your lotus feet and thus carry on the preaching of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, which you have established in this world for saving the most fallen conditioned souls. We hope you will give us the opportunity to fulfill your desires. Without your grace no one can have the chance of going back home, back to Godhead.

*bhavāpavargo bhramato yadā bhavej*  
*janasya tarhy acyuta sat-samāgamaḥ*  
*sat-saṅgamo yarhi tadaiva sad-gatau*  
*parāvareśe tvayi jāyate matiḥ*

**"O my Lord! O infallible Supreme Person! When a person wandering throughout the universe becomes eligible for liberation from material existence, he gets an opportunity to associate with devotees. When he associates with devotees, his attraction for You is awakened. You are the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the highest goal of the supreme devotees, and the Lord of the universe."**

The value of a moment's association with the pure devotee of the Lord cannot even be compared to the attainment of heavenly planets or liberation from matter, and what to speak of worldly benedictions in the form of material prosperity, which is for those who are meant for death. You are the *śakty-āveśa avatāra* of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself. Without being empowered by the Lord, who can preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world? O swanlike personality, we therefore pray that you engage us constantly in the thoughts of your lotus feet and your instructions. Unfortunately, we are so much fallen and wretched that our mind always runs toward material sense objects, thereby rejecting devotional service, and we are constantly kicked from one body to another, from one planet to another. And in this way, we are simply rotting in this material world from time immemorial. Now it is only by your mercy that we can come out of this rotting situation.

O beloved Gurudeva, you have given us the mission of carrying on the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya in South India. But we are afraid that it may not be possible to execute it without being fully empowered by the dust of your lotus feet. We pray unto the lotus feet of all Vaiṣṇavas to give us enough strength so that we can remain as your sincere servants, life after life, within the compound of devotional service, following the four regulative principles and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, tears are rolling out of our eyes, and our hearts are blocked with full feelings of your separation. Gurudeva, please reveal yourself to us in the form of your instructions, and thereby enable us to carry on your unlimited desires, life after life. Once again we fall unlimited times prostrated at your lotus feet, and we hope that you will empower us to preach the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu in every nook and corner of South India, out of your causeless mercy.

Thanking you,

*Your most fallen, unworthy, wretched servants,*

Śankhabhṛt dāsa brahmacārī / Ananta-padmanābha dāsa brahmacārī / Ātmatattva dāsa brahmacārī / Dhanvantari dāsa brahmacārī / Dayālu dāsa brahmacārī / Vaikuṇṭha dāsa / Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa

## Bangladesh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, master of masters, at your lotus feet all *mahātmās* come to take shelter. With great joy they serve those lotus feet in awe and reverence, being desirous to receive your benediction by accepting initiation and calling you Guru.

O teacher of teachers, you have revealed the purest knowledge, the most secret of all secrets,

*rāja-vidyā rāja-guhyaṁ pavitram idam uttamam*  
*pratyakṣāvagamaṁ dharmyaṁ su-sukhaṁ kartum avyayam*

Following your instructions, we are able to perceive the perfection of religion–love of Godhead–by direct realization of the self, thus becoming situated in our eternal constitutional position, performing devotional activities for the pleasure of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

O leader of leaders, you have fulfilled Śrī Caitanya's prophecy and are guiding a movement that is inundating every planet in the universe with pure love for Kṛṣṇa Now you have empowered us to drown everyone on earth with this *kṛṣṇa-prema,* and by your desire you have left to take up this mission elsewhere, following the request of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi.

O savior of saviors, you have rescued us from darkness and ignorant life, evoking sincerity and inspiring inspiration. Previously, we were the poorest of the poor, a burden to every living entity on the planet. But by your grace, we are now a benefit to every living entity, working only for their own well-being, according to your plan. We are on our way to Kṛṣṇa, by your mercy only, for He has accepted us into His service, and thereby you have made us the most fortunate of fortunate souls.

We devotees preaching here in Bangladesh are desirous of receiving your acceptance of our service. Although we are not qualified, please give us the qualification to represent you, and thereby convince all the people to surrender their lives to the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement. Though we are not intelligent, please give us the intelligence to preach with a compassionate heart; though we are not leaders, kindly guide us to make the right decisions to conquer this land; though our minds are lazy and restless, please bless us that we may become fixed up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to fulfill your desire to make Bangladesh a Vaiṣṇava nation. Now this country is in Muslim hands, but formerly it was the original Gaura-maṇḍala-bhūmi. So we are seeking the determination to stay here and preach until once again this land is returned to Śrī Caitanya!

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, saint of saints, the ever well-wisher of all living entities, we, the devotees of Bangladesh Yātrā, on this day of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, fall flat out at your lotus feet with great respect and beg to remain your eternal helpers. Hare Kṛṣṇa!

## Barcelona

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O Gurudeva, of all auspicious days, this is the most auspicious one, because by your appearance you have illuminated the darkness of this age of quarrel with the torchlight of knowledge. Lord Caitanya five hundred years ago distributed love of Godhead all over India, and He predicted that one day this love would be distributed all over the world, in every town and village, and you have come to satisfy this desire and prediction of the Lord. In a very short time, due to your purity and determination, you have done such great work that no one in the history of all the universe has ever, ever done before.

Your personality is so important that we will never be able to understand. Day by day we realize more and more your greatness. By enjoying the chanting of the holy name in *kīrtana,* by chanting *japa,* by reciting the Gāyatrī *mantra,* by performing devotional services, we see all our bad qualifications becoming purified, we feel our hearts becoming purified– day by day our illusion is gradually going away by following your instruction, and every day we are realizing more and more the purity of your love for Kṛṣṇa. Dear Gurudeva, you are like an island in the middle of the ocean of repeated birth and death, an island where everyone can take shelter, and your books are making these shelters so easily available. Just as Viṣṇu is expanding in the form of the Paramātmā to enter everyone's heart, similarly, you are expanding yourself in the form of your books to enter everyone's house to bring knowledge, blessing, and eternity.

You have given us a simple instruction–"Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy"–but illusion is always pushing us back, trying to make us stop chanting and go back to material life. But in your books you have put so much knowledge and instruction to help us that just by reading your books a nectar of knowledge is entering our ears, inundating our minds and hearts with a transcendental power that helps us to push back the illusion.

You have a great desire that your books enter into each and every house, and we are trying very hard to fulfill this desire of yours, but we are so fallen and devoid of strength due to our contamination, so we implore you to please bestow your mercy upon us. Please give us a drop of your love for Kṛṣṇa so that we will be strengthened to keep pushing on with this *saṅkīrtana* movement for the benefit of all the living entities.

With your great desire, you are giving to all living entities a chance to save themselves from this ocean of suffering. Please let us be surrendered tools in your hand to help you accomplish this difficult work for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day–the most glorious day–we beg to be able to remain your worthless servants.

## Berkeley

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

During the course of your appearance in the Western world, you made many statements which were very difficult to comprehend at the time. Now, however, with the passage of time and advancing devotional service, these things are gradually becoming apparent. You actually revolutionized every corner of our existence. The value of the cow–who in this society knows this simple truth? Cheating government leaders were always a target of your attacks. Many of us thought, "Surely there must be *some* leaders who do not want simply to exploit." But almost day by day, now, these leaders are being exposed, and even the common man has lost all confidence.

You always said that unless the people in general took to devotional service, there would be no way of averting another major world war. This is the way material nature relieves the burden of sinful activities. Now we see leading nations preparing, and it seems simply a matter of time.

Existence in this world simply consists of one type of suffering after another. Without your guidance, life would be unbearable. You appeared in this world to establish devotional service. You have elevated some of your disciples to the position *of guru,* and they are now sincerely continuing your work. Through your instructions and the initiating *gurus,* we are still able to feel the soothing rays and protection you provided. Please give us all the strength to remain eternally absorbed in devotional service.

*Your servants in Berkeley,*

## Bhaktivedanta Institute

*Dear, most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our worthless obeisances at your lotus feet. Our obeisances are worthless because they are only intermittent. We *should* be ceaselessly worshiping your lotus feet in utmost humility, but often we forget that our position is like that of insignificant insects swimming in the limitless ocean of your universal loving kindness. And with this forgetfulness comes forgetfulness of your lotus feet. Still, if you give us strength to carry on your mission, we will see how miniscule are our achievements compared with what you have done single-handedly. And as we strive to realize our desire to please you, we shall begin to understand a little of your true greatness.

This whole earth has become a battlefield, and you are the general who has drafted us into Lord Kṛṣṇa's army. Now we must constantly pray for your mercy so that we can shake off our deadly lethargy and awaken a fierce fighting spirit. You have ceased your earthly pastimes, but we do not feel that you have left us to fight alone. In your books, in your recorded talks, in your letters and personal instructions, in your eleven chief representatives, in all your disciples' hearts, you are personally present, guiding and enthusing us. We thus take Arjuna as our exemplar. On the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra, Lord Kṛṣṇa mounted his chariot and took the reins. Although Kṛṣṇa did not bear weapons for Arjuna, His auspicious presence ensured Arjuna's victory.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, since you are as good as Lord Kṛṣṇa, we fervently implore you to drive our chariot, for we are also faced with many *akṣauhiṇī* divisions, all well equipped with various kinds of dangerous weapons. We are very few, very inexperienced, and materially very insignificant compared to the atheists. Still, we are confident of victory, for you are guiding us.

You have said that this whole sinful culture, which has the godless material scientists as its high priests, can last only a few more years. Its leaders have no spiritual intelligence; they are engaged in unbeneficial, horrible works meant to destroy the world. How much longer will Kṛṣṇa allow this insane system to go on? Therefore, our only business is to help establish the glorious, Kṛṣṇa conscious alternative before it crumbles.

As Arjuna faced a seemingly invincible foe, Kṛṣṇa encouraged him, *mayaivaite nihitāḥ pūrvam eva nimitta-mātraṁ bhava savyasācin.* We understand that this godless society has already been destroyed by the force of time, the Lord's insurmountable *cakra,* and that we must simply become your instruments to bring in the golden age of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. If we fail to play our assigned part in destroying the atheistic science and philosophy that has caused so much suffering in the world, then someone else will take up the battle. But in any case, the so-called scientists and philosophers are already defeated.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is not possible to overestimate the importance of your mercy upon us. You have said, "If we can change the minds of the scientists, we can easily spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world." It is very wonderful that you have encouraged us in this way. But without your mercy, how is it possible for us to change the minds of the scientists? We are hardly able to change our *own* minds. After so many years of constant association with your pure devotees, how much spirit of loving devotion do we have? We should not be in illusion that material education has made us more glorious. Rather, it has pulled us down and qualified us only as bigger asses. Therefore, we are lost without your mercy. By nature and training, our minds will have to be engaged in *some* activity, but if by illusion we act under the spell of false ego–not hearing you–we shall be lost.

As our guiding principle, we take a verse you often quoted from *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

*idaṁ hi puṁsas tapasaḥ śrutasya vā*  
*sviṣṭasya sūktasya ca buddhi-dattayoḥ*  
*avicyuto ’rthaḥ kavibhir nirūpito*  
*yad-uttamaśloka-guṇānuvarṇanam*

**"Learned circles have positively concluded that the infallible purpose of the advancement of knowledge, namely austerities, study of the** ***Vedas,*** **sacrifice, chanting of hymns, and charity, culminates in the transcendental descriptions of the Lord, who is defined in choice poetry." (*****Bhāg*****. 1.5.22)**

You have commented that "Philosophy and science should be engaged to establish the glory of the Lord. Advanced people are eager to understand the Absolute Truth through the medium of science, and therefore great scientists should endeavor to prove the existence of the Lord on a scientific basis." These powerful words of yours will always remain in the forefront of our minds.

Certainly, our training and experience before becoming devotees was material. But we are followers of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, whom Lord Caitanya empowered to spread the science of *bhakti* all over the world, and who has established the principle of *yukta-vairāgya* as the highest principle of renunciation. We can thus perfect our lives by engaging everything–including our pathetic M.A.'s and Ph.D.'s–in the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa, with detachment and with full understanding of our relationship to Him.

When Svarūpa Dāmodara arrived at Bhaktivedanta Manor in August 1977, you told him, "It is not our business to condemn the advancement of knowledge. But by the advancement of knowledge to prove that there is no God–that is a waste of time. Just like we are taking advantage of scientific medicine and other things. That is good. But we are not denying the existence of God. That is not our business. People are suffering so much, only for this. *Sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam: this* knowledge we want to .spread. That is Kṛṣṇa consciousness." We shall never forget how you wept at this point, feeling intense compassion for the victims of the atheistic so-called science.

With whatever humility and sincerity we can muster, we fall at your lotus feet and beg forgiveness for our innumerable offenses. We pray that you empower us to preserve your indomitable spirit of dedication and compassion and to expand it throughout the world. Please give us great vigor and determination to engage every ounce of strength and intelligence in your service, and please give us loving devotion, so that in the midst of our activities, we never forget that we are simply your peons.

*Your fallen servants at the Bhaktivedanta Institute,*

Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa / Ādi-keśava Svāmī / Ravīndra-svarūpa dāsa / Sadāpūta dāsa / Mādhava dāsa / Dayādhara dāsa / Jñāna dāsa / Bhāgavata-darśana Ācārya dāsa / Garuḍa dāsa / Brahmatīrtha dāsa / Haladhara dāsa / Mahābuddhi dāsa / Vedavyāsa dāsa / Pūrṇānanda dāsa / Śyāmasundara dāsa / Śāśvata dāsa / Draviḍa dāsa / Khañjana-locana dāsa / Paṅkaja / Śrīkānta

## Bhubaneswar

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

**"My dear Parvati, there are different methods of worship, and out of all such methods, the worship of the Supreme Person is considered to be the highest; but even higher than the worship of the Lord is the worship of the Lord's devotee."**

Here Lord Śiva, who is known as Mahādeva, or the great god among the demigods, clearly indicates that even greater than all the demigods, including himself, is Lord Viṣṇu, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and that worship of Him is an essential fact. But even greater than worship of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is the worship of the Lord's pure devotees. The Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, also confirms this fact in the *Ādi Purāṇa,* where He addresses Arjuna thus: "My dear Pārtha, one who claims to be My devotee is not so. Only one who claims to be the devotee of My devotee is actually My devotee." So worship of the Lord's pure devotee is emphasized also by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, as the highest means of worshiping the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself.

A question may be raised here as to why even the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself holds the pure devotee in a more exalted position than Himself. The answer is that it is because of the pure devotee's exclusive interest in trying to engage every one of the living entities in the pure devotional service of the Lord for the Lord's full satisfaction and pleasure. The example can be cited here of the *gopīs.* The *gopīs'* full pleasure is in arranging different transcendental meetings for the spiritual pastimes of the Supreme Personality of Godhead and Rādhārāṇī. And Rādhārāṇī’s greatest pleasure is in arranging for the meetings of Her different associates and assistants with the Supreme Personality of Godhead for their spiritual pastimes. Each one takes greater pleasure in seeing the other satisfying the Lord in devotional service. Hence, it is because of this intense desire of the devotees of the Lord to satisfy the every whim and wish of the Supreme Personality of Godhead that the Supreme Personality of Godhead holds them as His very heart. *Sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyam.* Because the Supreme Personality of Godhead holds the pure devotees as so dear to Himself, and because He considers them worshipable (as He has exemplified by worshiping the great pure devotee Sudāmā Vipra), when the pure devotees know this to be the most intense desire and exalted interest of the Lord, they are always eager to satisfy this main interest of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. That is, they desire to serve the pure devotees by assisting them in their service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The greatest of all those services is to create more pure devotees to engage in the transcendental service of the Personality of Godhead. Therefore the Supreme Lord states in the *Bhagavad-gītā,*

*ya idaṁ paramaṁ guhyaṁ mad-bhakteṣv abhidhāsyati*  
*bhaktiṁ mayi parāṁ kṛtvā mām evaiṣyaty asaṁśayaḥ*

**"For one who explains the supreme secret to the devotees, devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come back to Me."**

*na ca tasmān manuṣyeṣu kaścin me priya-kṛttamaḥ*  
*bhavitā na ca me tasmād anyaḥ priyataro bhuvi*

**"There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, nor will there ever be one more dear."**

So the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, holds the preacher of the transcendental message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the fallen, conditioned souls to be the most dear and confidential of all His servants. Therefore, it is the prime interest of all pure devotees to deliver the fallen, conditioned souls, who are sunk so deeply in the muck and mire of this material world, back to home, back to Godhead. One who performs this activity is known as the *ācārya,* and Kṛṣṇa says in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* Eleventh Canto,

*ācāryaṁ māṁ vijānīyān nāvanmanyeta karhicit*  
*na martya-buddhyāsūyeta sarva-deva-mayo guruḥ*

**"One should know the** ***ācārya*** **as Myself and never disrespect him in any way. One should not envy him, thinking him an ordinary man, for he is the representative of all the demigods."**

The *dcdrya* is to be taken as Kṛṣṇa; that is, because he is the transcendental ambassador of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, because he is delivering the transcendental message of the Lord without changing it in the *guru-paramparā* system, then he is to be given the same greeting, the same respect, as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The *ācārya,* being the ambassador of the Lord, is empowered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead to act just like the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Therefore there are so many transcendental qualities invested in the *dcdrya.* The *dcdrya* is known as *tri-kāla-jña.* This means that he can understand time in three phases–past, present, and future. An example of this is given in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* Canto Four, Chapter Eight, text 25.

*nāradas tad upākarṇya jñātvā tasya cikīrṣitam*  
*spṛṣṭvā mūrdhany agha-ghnena pāṇinā prāha vismitaḥ*

**"The great sage Nārada overheard this news, and understanding all the activities of Dhruva Mahārāja, he was struck with wonder. He approached Dhruva and, touching his head with his all-virtuous hand, spoke as follows."**

Here it is shown that Nārada Muni was overhearing the discussions between Sunīti and her son, the great devotee Dhruva Mahārāja. Someone may ask how he was able to overhear these topics, and the answer is given by Śrīla Prabhupāda in the purport: because he is *tri-kāla-jña,* or so powerful that he can understand the past, present, and future of everyone's heart. Śrīla Prabhupāda explains and clarifies this statement by pointing out that because the Supreme Personality of Godhead is present in everyone's heart, then He always understands when a living entity is serious about entering into devotional service, and therefore He sends His representative. Because the pure devotee is in contact with the Supreme Personality of Godhead in His all-pervasive form as Paramātmā, and because the Paramātmā knows everything about everyone, everywhere, at every moment, then there is nothing hidden from the pure devotee. The pure devotee may know anything that he wants to know, at any time, about anyone, anywhere. So this is the power which is invested in the *ācārya* who is on the great mission of reclaiming the fallen, conditioned souls back to home, back to Godhead. So even though Nārada was in a distant place, he could understand that his disciple was waiting, and therefore he came and delivered to him transcendental knowledge. Similarly, we have seen in our experience with our divine master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, that although we were on the other side of the planet, still he could understand that his disciples were waiting for him there in America, and so by the order of his spiritual master, who is the external manifestation of Paramātmā, and by his all-powerful position of understanding past, present, and future, he came to the Western world to deliver us from the muck and mire of material existence. Therefore it is said in *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde paya bhakti-latā-bīja:* "By the grace of the spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa, one can get the seed of transcendental devotional service." The *ācārya* is so powerful that even staunch Māyāvādīs can be converted by his transcendental influence. This is the grace of the Supreme Lord and the *guru.*

Just like we see in the activities of the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, when He went to speak with the Māyāvādī *sannyāsīs* in Benares, He was able to convert all of them into becoming pure devotees of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and to chanting the holy names, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. How He did it was most wonderful, because when He first came to the meeting, He could understand that they were not qualified to hear the transcendental message of the Lord, and therefore they would not be able to change their stonelike hearts. So the Lord played a trick upon them. After He washed His lotus feet at the bathing place before entering the house. He sat down there, at the spot where one washes his feet. This is considered to be a very dirty place. When the head of the Māyāvādī *sannyāsīs,* Prakāśānanda Sarasvatī, noted that the Supreme Lord was sitting there, he became very much disturbed, and wanting to show proper etiquette and respect to another *sannyāsī,* he came forward and put his hand out to grab the lotuslike hand of the Supreme Lord, and he requested Him humbly to please come forward and take His proper seat in the assembly of *sannyāsīs.* By catching hold of the hand of the Supreme Lord and escorting Him forward to His seat, Prakāśānanda Sarasvatī performed *ajñāta-sukṛti,* or unknown devotional service, which then qualified him to hear the transcendental message of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and thus his stonelike heart was changed from the conclusion of Māyāvāda to the conclusion of worshiping the Supreme Personality of Godhead in His transcendental lotuslike form of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya. Similarly, we have noted in our divine master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, how, when he came to the Western world to reclaim the fallen, conditioned souls from the dregs of material life, he found that we were so staunchly saturated in the Māyāvāda and voidist conception of spiritual life (where there was hardly any conception of spiritual life at all) that he would have to trick us into performing different services to his lotus feet, and in this way he engaged us in performing so many little services to him, by which we became qualified to hear the transcendental message of the Supreme Personality of Godhead–by distributing *prasāda,* by asking people to type his manuscripts, by getting us to do other little services within the *āśrama* and the temple. Gradually, little by little, we were becoming more qualified to hear the transcendental message of the Lord. In this way, he was able to change the stonelike hearts of the Māyāvādī and voidist philosophers of the Western world and make them into flowerlike devotees of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa.

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**"Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism."**

When the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu heard that the gross materialists Jagāi and Mādhāi had committed a great offense against Lord Nityānanda, He was prepared to come to the spot and immediately kill the offenders. By the persuasion of Lord Nityānanda, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu benedicted the two brothers by not killing them but killing their demoniac mentality by engaging them in the chanting of the holy name of the Lord, the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra–* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. This was the grace of Śrī Caitanya, that He distributed the chanting of the holy name of the Lord freely to all living entities, thus converting them from *asura* to *sura.* Thus we have seen in our divine master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, this great potency given to him by the Supreme Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, that wherever he went, simply by chanting these holy names Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, then the fallen conditioned souls had their demoniac mentalities transformed into the mentalities of divine servants of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa.

It is stated in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* that Vāsudeva Datta, who was the incarnation of Prahlāda Mahārāja, was willing to stay in the material world and suffer all the sinful reactions of all the living entities so that all of the living entities could go back home, back to Godhead. This is the prime example of the compassionate nature of an *ācārya* and great devotee of the Lord. Similarly, we have seen in the character of our divine master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, how he took every risk to come to the Western world at a late age and preach the divine message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. While traveling on the boat to the Western world, he nearly died of a heart attack. While living in New York City, many times he was unable to get proper living facilities. Once he was greatly harassed by one of the persons in one of the places in which he was living, and had to leave the house with no shelter for the night. But by the mercy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, he was able to get shelter that evening. Undertaking all types of difficulties, all types of discomforts at a late age. His Divine Grace's only concern was, "How can I save the fallen living entities from this suffering of material nature, from their separation from their service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead?" Therefore, he was willing to undertake all types of suffering in order to accomplish this end. He also took on many thousands of disciples, and for each disciple he had to take on all of the sinful reactions and had to suffer for them. This is the divine mercy of our master, that he underwent so much suffering for the deliverance of us fallen, conditioned souls, his insincere disciples. It is stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.1.6),

*tvayā khalu purāṇāni setihāsāni cānagha*  
*ākhyātāny apy adhītāni dharma-śāstrāṇi yāny uta*

**"Respected Sūta Gosvāmī, you are completely free from all vice. You are well versed in all the scriptures famous for religious life, and in the** ***Purāṇas*** **and the histories as well, for you have gone through them under proper guidance and have also explained them."**

Śrīla Prabhupāda points out in the purport that no one should sit on the *vyāsāsana* who is not spotless in character or who cannot thoroughly explain all the revealed scriptures after having thoroughly been through them himself, such as the *Vedas,* the *Purāṇas,* and the histories such as the *Mahābhārata* and *Rāmāyaṇa,* and all such scriptures. Then in the next verse,

*yāni veda-vidāṁ śreṣṭho bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ*  
*anye ca munayaḥ sūta parāvara-vido viduḥ*

**"Being the eldest learned Vedāntist, O Sūta Gosvāmī, you are acquainted with the knowledge of Vyāsadeva, who is the incarnation of Godhead, and you also know other sages who are versed in all kinds of physical and metaphysical knowledge."**

It is stated by Śrīla Prabhupāda in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* commentary that besides Śrīla Vyāsadeva's natural commentary on *Vedānta-sūtra,* there are also other sages who are authors of six different philosophical systems, namely Gautama, Kaṇāda, Kapila, Patañjali, Jamini, and Aṣṭāvakra. Theism is explained completely in the *Vedānta-sūtra,* whereas in other systems of philosophical speculations, practically no mention is given to the ultimate cause of all causes. One can sit on the *vyāsāsana* only after being conversant in all systems of philosophy, so that one can present fully the theistic views of the *Bhāgavatam* in defiance of all other systems. We have seen you, our divine master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, again and again explain and expound upon the theistic values and teachings of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in defiance of all other philosophical systems, not only of the Eastern philosophers, but of all of the Western philosophers also, teaching us the histories, the stories, and the explanations of the Vedic literature–the *Mahābhārata,* the *Rāmāyaṇa,* the *Purāṇas;* through innumerable lecture halls, auditoriums, temples, pandal programs, and again and again in the purports of your wonderful books. You have expounded upon the science of devotional service, and after thoroughly defeating all other oppositions of all other philosophies, and all other ideologies, and all other isms, and all other religions, you have clearly enunciated that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and pure devotional service to His lotus feet is the only means by which to attain Him, and that to get that devotional service one must go through the divine intermediary and ambassador, the spiritual master.

So on this day, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, we must bow to you with great awe and reverence, love and devotion, offering respect and veneration to our divine master, who has exemplified in every way all the qualities of the *ācārya,* who has manifested in every way all the transcendental qualities which decorate such a great person as the *ācārya.* And we beg on this day that, although we are quite fallen and quite incapable and unfit to be your servants and disciples, somehow or other you may allow us to take shelter at your lotus feet and make advancement on the path back to home, back to Godhead.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*

Because, as it is stated by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura,

**"There is no way by which we can attain the shelter of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, without getting the mercy of the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead, the spiritual master."**

Our divine master, His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, has so kindly come to give us this transcendental message of Kṛṣṇa, of *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and has very mercifully entrusted us with the mission of carrying on this transcendental movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu through every nook and corner of the world. Although we are perfectly unfit and unqualified, being lower than the most sinful living entities in the universe, still our spiritual master, being very kind and merciful upon us, and seeing no other way for us to be delivered, has engaged us in trying to deliver the fallen living entities from the fallen condition of material existence. He has made it his order to us that we must make it our life and soul to preach this transcendental message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Therefore any success that any of us have in any respect can only be measured as his unlimited mercy upon us.

*yogyata-vicāre, kichu nahi pāi,*  
*tomāra karuṇā sāra*  
*karuṇā na hoile, kāndiyā kāndiyā,*  
*prāṇa nā rakhibo āra*

"Upon examining my value," Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura states in his great poem *Gurudeva,* "I find that I have none." So actually this is our position. Then Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda states, "Your mercy is essential to me." Actually this is the position of the disciple, because the mercy of the spiritual master is all that we are made of. We are the creation of his mercy. "Therefore, if you are not merciful," he says, "then I will constantly weep, and I will not maintain my life any longer." Therefore this is the real attitude of the disciple. The disciple feels that if he cannot carry out the order of the spiritual master, then rather he should be dead. Unfortunately we are such lowly and unqualified snakes that we cannot even have this feeling. Being too much attached to our senses and our bodily comforts, we are unable to feel this desire within our hearts– either to execute the desire of our spiritual master or to perish. So as the great poet Rūpa Gosvāmī prays in *The Nectar of Devotion, Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu,* then we also pray to you, our divine master, that "I have no love for Kṛṣṇa, nor for the causes of developing love of Kṛṣṇa, namely hearing and chanting, and the process of *bhakti-yoga,* by which one is always thinking of Kṛṣṇa by fixing His lotus feet in the heart, is also lacking in me. As far as philosophical knowledge and pious works are concerned, I don't see any opportunity for me to execute such activities, but above all I am not even born of a nice family."

Therefore, we must pray to you, our divine master, that we are simply wishing and hoping that some way or other we may be able to approach your divine lotus feet, and this hope is giving us great pain, because we think ourselves quite incompetent and unfit to approach that transcendental goal of life. Therefore on this, your transcendental appearance day, our dear spiritual master, we are offering our most humble obeisances to you with all awe and veneration and great respect, and we are begging you that somehow or other you may make us qualified to execute some more devotional service to you, so that we may become qualified to execute some more devotional service to you in the future coming of the crores of the births that we may spend here in this material world.

*Our most respectful obeisances from your humble servants in Bhubaneswar Yātrā,*

Gaura Govinda Svāmī / Bhāgavata dāsa brahmacārī (Bhakti-śāstri) / Lagudie dāsa brahmacārī / Caitanya-siṁha dāsa adhikārī / Deva-dharma dāsa adhikārī / Amala-harināma dāsa brahmacārī / Trimala Bhaṭṭa dāsa brahmacārī / Jyotsnā dāsa brahmacārī / Lalitā-Govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛtāgama dāsa brahmacārī / Vaiṣṇava dāsa brahmacārī / Vijaya Kumar / Vṛndāvana / Ajaya / Sravana Kumar / Racitāmbara-devī dāsī / Ambikā-devī dāsī / Jaya Rādhe / Digpati dāsa adhikārī / Ravi Narayana Mahunty / Yudhisthira Karana / Yudhisthira Karana

## Bolivia

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories unto you, the most dear associate of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa.

We are feeling unable to glorify you even a little properly. We are feeling unable to do at least a little of what we should do, considering what you have done for us. We are feeling unable to go out in the streets and yell about your glories, for just a small drop of your highly elevated transcendental pastimes, when heard by even the most degraded human being, can liberate him at once. We are feeling unable to realize who you really are and how you manifested yourself within this material world. We are feeling unable to appreciate and consider how fortunate we are for having had the chance to meet with your *Bhagavad-gītā* and your holy message. Please, now that we are feeling so fallen and unworthy, we want to beg you to maintain us in your service and in the service of your older disciples, for that is the only way for a spirit soul to be safe and protected from the pangs of material existence.

The material conception of life is so horrible. We fear very much, considering even a moment of your separation painful; that is why we humbly beg you to engage our minds always in thinking of and being absorbed in you and your teachings. We have surrendered unto you, at least officially, so you are the master, you are the controller. Please do not ever let us contemplate for even a second the external energy, and use us fully as instruments to attain whatever you may want.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our asking is very important and urgent. Please always give us someone to preach to.

*Your fallen servants in Bolivia,*  
Prahlādanātha dāsa / Dāru Kṛṣṇa dāsa

## Bombay

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Empowered Ācāryadeva,  
Bestower of the divine benediction of *kṛṣṇa-prema,*  
Refuge of the world,  
Deliverer of fallen souls,  
Pure devotee of the Lord,

On this day we join together in remembrance of you. You worked very hard to establish the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī Cultural Center at Hare Kṛṣṇa Land in Juhu. It is by your will only that this gorgeous towering complex has arisen to show the world the completeness of spiritual life. You have established the Lord in His different forms as Śrī Śrī Rādhā- Rāsavihārījī-Lalitā-Viśākhā, Śrī Śrī Guru-Gaurāṅga, and Śrī Śrī Sītā-Rāma-Lakṣmaṇa-Hanumān. Every week tens of thousands of people thrill at Their *darśana* and the opulent standard of worship established by Your Divine Grace. The palatial guesthouse and *prasāda* restaurant are an open invitation to the world to come and enjoy the ecstasy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The Bhaktivedanta Institute here is proof that *bhakti* is not a sentimental process, but rather the platform of real, scientific knowledge. Within the theater, cultural performances reveal that God may be worshiped by dramatic presentations, and you always encouraged your disciples to undertake this mode of conveying your message to the general public. Now, by your grace, a Gurukula will be coming up here to give fortunate young boys the opportunity to imbibe Kṛṣṇa consciousness from the very childhood. Before Your Divine Grace ever went to America, you had your lotus eyes on this piece of property. And when you returned to India, Lord Kṛṣṇa gave it you, but not without your first proving tirelessly to Him that establishing Hare Kṛṣṇa Land was among your dearmost desires.

Hare Kṛṣṇa Land is an incarnation of you, and everywhere we look we see manifestations of your incomparable mercy and unfathomably deep intellect. You have carved a palatial temple out of crude rock, yet you never cared to live here and enjoy the fruits of your work. You gave Hare Kṛṣṇa Land to the world of devotees as a profound proof of your selfless devotion to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī. Indeed, when They had only a small makeshift hut to stand in, you used to stand before the Deity and say, "My dear Sir, kindly stand here. I shall take care of everything for You." Therefore on this day we feel very grateful for all you have done for us. We join together in offering you our humble obeisances repeatedly.

We, the Hare Kṛṣṇa Land-vāsīs, offer our most humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa 108 Śrī Śrīmad A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda

–who is the shelter of all lost souls throughout the universe.

–who never cared for his own personal safety or comfort when executing the order of his spiritual master.

–who is a pure devotee of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī in the love-filled groves of Vraja.

–whose life and soul was, is, and ever shall be the order and instruction of his spiritual master.

–who never lived for a fraction of a moment outside the command of his spiritual master.

–who shall be remembered always as the redeemer from the thralldom of Kali-yuga and inaugurator of the era of Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava-ism as predicted by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

–who regarded Bombay as his office, Vṛndāvana-dhāma as his home, and Śrīdhāma Māyāpur as his place of worship.

–whose *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* goes on making more and more devotees, while other commentaries by ambitious so-called *yogis* and scholars continue to collect dust because nobody cares for the loose ramblings of conceited fools.

–whose translation and commentary on *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* is a repository of absolute wisdom as seen from a million angles of vision, and which is a passageway to Goloka-dhāma.

–whose rendering of *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* completed on Śrī Ramā Ekādaśī at Hare Kṛṣṇa Land, gives the clearest insight into how one can enter into confidential loving devotional service of the Lord.

–who never lost a debate and who silenced atheists, antagonists, false religionists, Māyāvādīs, and even vicious animals in the dress of men, with infallible logic from a lightning-swift mind.

–who is a reservoir of goodness, mercy, compassion, truthfulness, fearlessness, intelligence, and all other qualities found in the personalities of the most intimate confidential associates of Śrī Hari.

–who brought Kṛṣṇa from the spiritual sky in the form of *arcd-vigraha* and established Him in Bombay as Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihārījī, in Vṛndāvana as Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, In Māyāpur as Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, in New York as Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, in Chicago as Śrī Kisora-Kisori, in Los Angeles as Śrī Rukmiṇī-Dvārakādhīśa, in San Francisco as Śrī Jagannātha-Subhadrā-Baladeva, in Honolulu as Śrī Pañca-tattva, in London as Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, and in the hearts of your pure disciples, whose lives have become now perfect by your causeless grace alone.

–whose Hare Kṛṣṇa Land project has raised the eyebrows of everyone in India.

–whose feet are adored by the celestial *devatās,* who long to be able to complete in some distant life what you were able to accomplish in a dozen earthly years.

–who has three maidservants in the divine personages of Śrī Lakṣmī, Śrī Sarasvatī, and the goddess of liberation.

–who withheld oceans of spiritual bliss within, which sometimes broke the dam of even your mighty spiritual strength and manifested as tears of love, cleansing the hearts of all with the tidal wave of Hare Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana.*

–who despised the duplicity of show-bottle transcendentalists that cheat the gullible public with stagecraft *yoga* and well-rehearsed *samādhi* performances, and exposed them all, one by one.

–who brushed aside the empty theories of atheistic scientists and who established the Bhaktivedanta Institute to prove that the real scientist must be a Kṛṣṇa conscious man.

–who translated *varṇāśrama-dharma* into real life by creating *brāhmaṇa* Vaiṣṇavas out of *mlecchas,* who in turn are able to teach brahminical culture to advocates of "caste by birth."

–who watered the root of the tree of Lord Caitanya's movement and produced fruits and flowers from barren limbs.

–who escorted Lord Jagannātha down Main St. U.S.A., Europe, and India, making sure that millions feasted on His remnants to their full satisfaction.

–who performed the inauguration of a coming golden age, which will give inestimable benefit to countless billions upon billions of lost *jīva* souls.

–who relaxed the burden of work load on the shoulders of Lord Yamarāja by teaching each and all of us how to stay far from the boundaries of his eerie realm.

–who saw everyone as Kṛṣṇa's eternal servant and racked his brain to engage everyone in the Lord's service.

–who, in the words of one renowned scholar, "filled his pen with fire and wrote in such a way as to pour molten lead onto the heads of the leaders of the nations of the earth, who are destined to die their deaths if they don't care to take his advice."

–who filled the world with spiritual literatures "by hook or by crook," so that millions of souls can be delivered back home, back to Godhead.

–who left his family to find one moonlike devotee and found many moons of his own creation, each capable of illuminating the nocturnal sky.

–whose International Society for Krishna Consciousness is virtually the only bona fide religious movement in the world.

–who showed the future of the earth by establishing New Vṛndāvana, Gītā-nāgarī, New Talavan, New Māyāpur, etc., which are based on agriculture, the cow, and *kṛṣṇa-bhakti.*

–who is the shelter of the sincere, and without whom we, your disciples, would be like so many lost splinters floating uselessly on the gigantic ocean of *may a.*

Please continue to guide us to work as a united society infused with spiritual grace under the guidance of your books, your empowered representatives, and learned disciples whose hearts and souls are bent upon executing your divine mission as a family founded upon love and trust.

## Boston

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

If it were not for your causeless mercy, what would be the plight for us helpless fallen souls? There is no limit to how degraded we would have become, being forced to engage in all varieties of sinful activities, paving our way toward hell. But by the intervention of Your Divine Grace, we have been rescued from the abyss of material degradation. In fact, you have created a future for us "as brilliant as the sun. You alone are responsible for our great fortune.

It was *you* who coaxed us away from the barbarous habit of animal-eating by showing us the art of feasting on spiritual foods, sumptuous *kṛṣṇa-prasāda.* It was *you* who freed us from the morass of mental speculation by forcefully enunciating Kṛṣṇa's conclusive truth of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is.* It was *you* who delivered us from the shackles of materialistic sex life by allowing us to reside in the relishable association of the Lord's loving servitors. And it was *you* who replaced our poisonous addictions with the eternal intoxicating ecstasy of *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana.* Who could fathom the ocean of mercy you have bestowed upon such apparently condemned creatures as ourselves? Whenever we think of the matchless gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you have so kindly given us, we chant "Jaya Prabhupāda!" in great love, and dance jubilantly, without any consideration.

We are beginning to understand how tremendously indebted we are to you. Out of gratefulness, we feel a strong desire to somehow repay you. We are, however, just like children who beg from the parents in order to purchase some gift. Is it possible for us to give you something without first receiving it from you and further increasing our debt? Perhaps we should boldly approach Kṛṣṇa directly and beg from Him the ability to assist you in carrying out your mission. We might pray, "Dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, You are the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Please be merciful upon us some day soon, so that we may act in total disregard of this material body and fully engage in fulfilling the desire of our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda...." But how can anyone approach Kṛṣṇa independently of His pure devotee? Or maybe we can simply offer you our love. But then again, we are reminded that this love has been awakened only by your blessed association.

We must finally conclude that your mercy is all that we are made of. Being bereft of all hope of ever fulfilling our obligation, we humbly submit this request at your lotus feet: please inspire and empower us to successfully spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, enabling us to fulfill your actual desire.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are praying that we may never again be so foolish as to think that we can relinquish our eternal indebtedness to you. Please accept us as menial servants, forever dependent on your mercy.

*Your disciples and granddisciples at Boston 1SKCON,*

## Brazil

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept the *daṇḍavats* of all your disciples and of all the disciples of your disciple Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami serving you here in Brazil at your transcendental lotus feet. There is not much we have to offer to you, since your order was to take over the world and make everybody aware of Kṛṣṇa's eternal kingdom. Because of our impurity we have not yet accomplished what you have ordered us to do. But now we have in Brazil the following temples:

In Sao Paulo we have the Deities Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Their Lordships Jagannātha, Subhadrā, and Balarāma. Also, we have here your Portuguese BBT, the Spiritual Sky company, and our biggest *saṅkīrtana* town in Brazil. In New Gokula we have our first ISKCON-owned project in South America. This beautiful farm belongs to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, Śrī Sītā-Rāma-Lakṣmaṇa-Hanumān, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. We have started here our Gurukula for Brazil and also a doll project. In Rio de Janeiro we have a little preaching center in the heart of the city and a house outside, where Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Naṭarāja reside. In Salvador Bahia we have a nice temple in the city and a nice little farm in a city close by called Feira de Santana. This year we want to invite Jagannātha Svāmī, Subhadra, and Balarama to give their mercy to the *mlecchas* here. In Recife and Belo Horizonte we have nice centers where we can install Gaura-Nitāi this year. Just recently opened were our preaching centers in Porto Alegre, Curitiba, and in Petropolis, as well as a restaurant in Brasilia.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only by your causeless mercy that we are able to move anywhere. Yesterday we were still lying in the streets, useless rejecters and also rejected. In that agnostic state you came and picked us up. Just as a touchstone can turn metal into gold, you astonished the whole world, and especially the caste *brāhmaṇas* and caste *gosvāmīs,* that by coming in contact with you, only by hearing about you or reading a book of yours (Śrīla Prabhupāda never came to Brazil in his *vapuḥ* form), these *mleccha* offsprings have become the sternest preachers of the *sanātana-dharma* in the whole world, so strict that even the idea of bheedis, coffee, and tea causes them to spit. Śrīla Prabhupāda, whatever your plan is, whatever you want us to do, these bodies given to us by Kṛṣṇa's arrangement are yours now. You must have some plan for delivering the world. Most probably you are staying with Lord Caitanya and all the Gosvāmīs, engaged in the confidential pastimes of *prema-bhakti* distribution. Whatever may be, we were not able to associate very much with your kindness, therefore our hearts feel broken if we think that we will not be able to regain that cooling shade of your lotus feet. Let us go wherever you want us to go, but please let us be always protected by the dust of your feet.

We cannot really declare that we love you, since ninety percent of our time is simply spent in our material bodily consciousness; but at least we have more love for you than for anybody else in this world. We have left our family, so-called friends, material environment, drugs, gambling– everything we throw away on your order. Now please kindly let us remain in your party, the only party full of knowledge, eternity, and bliss; the only party which can save the world; the only party which challenges the influence of Kali; the only party of the pure *caitanya-bhakti* mood; the only party of the worship of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa; the only party which will certainly end up in Goloka Vṛndāvana. Until we can meet you again we will simply keep chanting madly, Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants of ISKCON Brazil,

## Buenos Aires

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You are Lord Caitanya's foremost representative and most intimate associate. So He sent you to this universe to deliver all the conditioned souls through this process of the chanting of the holy names–Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. This is your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and because we are your sons, it is also ours. The father has two types of sons: those who only play in his garden and those who actually want to help him in his work and take up his mission as their life and soul.

So we beg to Lord Caitanya to be this second type of son. May our only desire and endeavor be to spread your movement, Śrīla Prabhupāda, all over this universe, eternally. May we become your perfect instruments in pleasing Lord Caitanya by successfully spreading this movement everywhere! This is our only desire. Please fulfill this desire. We desire no other benediction, so please give us this benediction! Let us distribute tons of books and make millions of *bhaktas*! You are Lord Caitanya's best friend, and if you ask Him, He will surely give us this boon. We desire nothing else.

Now we want to open a new temple and start preaching again in Argentina. It will not be so easy, because there are so many Rākṣasa demons there, especially the president, and they hate this movement so much. But we have full faith that you are the real president, controller of this country, because Lord Caitanya is in their hearts and He is controlled by your love for Him. So we do not think it will be difficult for you to establish Lord Caitanya's position here as the Supreme Personality of Godhead and make them all happy by their surrendering unto Him. Let this whole country become Kṛṣṇa conscious by your mercy. We only beg to be your instruments in this, your wonderful preaching pastime.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always giving Lord Caitanya the highest pleasure by your preaching pastimes; please may we now enter into those pastimes once and for all and eternally serve your lotus feet by spreading this movement. Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda!!!

*Your worthless servants of Buenos Aires Mandira,*

Dharmānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Kaivalya-svarūpa dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛṣṇa-kiśora dāsa brahmacārī / Oṁkāra dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Carlitos / Bhakta Enrique /

## Calcutta

*Dear Gurudeva,*

Only when one is able to get out of the clutches of hypocrisy will he be able to recognize you in the form of this movement. Once you said, "I am ISKCON." Yes, you are ISKCON. You are the books, you are the sound vibrations of our preaching, you are our taking *prasāda.* You are the temples, you are our very endeavor and determination and the ultimate success. You are the association of devotees and the *gurus.* No hypocrite will ever understand.

*yā niśā sarva-bhūtānāṁ tasyāṁ jāgarti saṁyamī*  
*yasyāṁ jāgrati bhūtāni sā niśā paśyato muneḥ*

**"What is night for all beings is the time of awakening for the self-controlled; and the time of awakening for all beings is night for the introspective sage." (Bg. 2.69)**

For the materialistic man you are not present. But for your devotees it is an ever-fresh discovery of your delicate presence. You are fully cognizant and aware of everything going on in your movement, because you yourself are the form of preaching. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us by letting us always remain in this movement, because in this way we'll be in you, and never lost again.

*Your servants in Calcutta,*

## Carrier

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We are approaching your lotus feet, begging for your mercy and engaging in the eternal activity of trying to recount your glories.

Someone who does not know your position as the most dear servant of the Supreme Personality of Godhead would be bewildered and skeptical that anyone could do as much as you have done. A worldwide movement with so many temples, farms, and schools, and so many transcendental books and preachers, and so many conditioned souls rescued by your mercy. We accept that, as Kṛṣṇa's intimate servitor, you can do anything, and we are also awestruck by what you have done.

As far as New Talavan farm is concerned, this project was begun on your order, built on your divine instructions, and continues to grow by your mercy. You instructed us to make this farm a living example of the Kṛṣṇa conscious way of life explained in your books. You have taught how all classes of men can be engaged, making a conducive atmosphere for Kṛṣṇa consciousness to grow. *Varṇāśrama-dharma.* This process of simple living and high thinking provides everyone the necessities of life and time to engage in developing God consciousness. You are so compassionate. You knew that by ourselves we would not be able to make a peaceful society, but simply by following your instructions we are doing it. By your grace the people of Kali-yuga can be elevated from animal life to human society– *brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas, vaiśyas,* and *śūdras–* people who will be able to understand the value of becoming Kṛṣṇa's devotees.

Cow protection is a service you gave us, not only out of compassion for the animals, but also for the so-called human beings. Cow protection is essential for a peaceful human society. The cow gives the brain-building miracle food–milk. She is the symbol of religiosity, and milk is liquid religiosity. Many people cannot take to spiritual life because they are too sinful and their lives too chaotic. If people could be induced to just stop slaughtering the cow, they would be saved from so many, many horrible sinful reactions such as war. We are trying to attract them to eating *prasāda* and drinking milk instead of eating cows, so they will become purified and find the shelter of your lotus feet.

Actually, by surrendering to you and following your instructions we become free. You've taught us that to be too dependent on others saps life's energy. And especially to depend on this modern society for the necessities of life is illusory, because this so-called modern "civilization" cannot last. It is temporary. By instructing us how to be self-sufficient, you have freed us from a life of useless, artificial "necessities" and endless frustrations. You teach actual self-sufficiency, which means to depend on what is real–Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa's pure devotees, Kṛṣṇa's cows, and Kṛṣṇa's land. You have freed us from dependence on gasoline, cinemas, grocery stores, slaughterhouses, T.V., electric can-openers, etc.–an endless list of things that deviate people from spiritual life.

At the same time you have taught us to take what there is and use it for Kṛṣṇa. That way you have made all of our activities transcendentally pleasing and useful. For example, all the things that people consider to be "culture"–such as art, music, drama, science, and literature–materially are culture for demons. But by your touch they become spiritual and bring people to Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, we will be glorifying Kṛṣṇa's pastimes in music, drama, paintings, and dioramas. Marble and wood carvers will decorate Kṛṣṇa's temple. Metalworkers and seamstresses decorate the Deities. The chef cooks for the Lord, and our transcendental waiters distribute the cuisine as *prasāda.* And our authors preach in the *New Talavan Review,* to attract conditioned souls to come and benefit from your wonderful program.

Just as you provided everything we have now, you also provided for the future of ISKCON. And in providing for the future of this movement, you also provided for the future of everyone on the planet. From the Gurukula school system that you established will come the purified preachers of the future. We have been born and raised in rascaldom and were trained up in nonsense before we came to you. We are not very qualified receivers or distributors of your mercy. But these children and their children, who have been born and raised in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and are trained up by you in the Gurukula, will be very qualified. They will be trained up in the art of loving Kṛṣṇa. They will know how to serve the order of the spiritual master and they will be able to save everyone by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness further and further.

Just see. How wonderful it is. You are the root cause of all the activity going on in New Talavan, what to speak of the rest of worldwide ISKCON. I know this is inadequate, but thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us, the cows, and everyone else the shade of your lotus feet. Perhaps if we dedicate every minute of this lifetime to rendering you service, it will be a small token of gratitude.

*ISKCON New Talavan Community, Carriere, Mississippi,*

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Now that our eyes are covered by *māyā,* we cannot see you. Due to your causeless mercy you appeared in front of our eyes and spoke about the spiritual sky, where Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is performing His eternal pastimes with His eternal associates. And you taught us that that spiritual sky is our real home, and that Kṛṣṇa is our real father and friend. How fortunate we are to receive this absolute knowledge from you, who are the dearmost associate of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda.

Unless one is extremely fortunate, one cannot see you, cannot hear you or touch you–for seeing you is seeing the spiritual sky, hearing you is hearing Lord Kṛṣṇa's flute, and touching you is touching the lotus feet of the Lord. What made us worthy of such great fortune? O beloved spiritual master, we were not at all worthy. It is simply your causeless mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, when we were groping and stumbling in the darkness of this material world, you took us by the hand and made us stand and showed us how to walk toward the spiritual sky, just the way a mother shows her small child how to walk. But we are so weak, so fallen, that we don't understand the urgency of getting out of this world of miseries and going home, back to Godhead. Our condition is very much like that of the child being shown how to walk: as soon as the mother's grasp is taken away, he forgets the serious occupation of walking and carries on with his play.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! We are so unfortunate and weak. Being separated from you, our hearts do not ache and tears do not come streaming down from our eyes. Dear lord and master, please, by your transcendental potency, make our hard hearts melt, and release the tears from our eyes, so that they may flow down to wash your lotus feet. Only then will we be able to hear the transcendental sound vibration Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, and then only will the sound spread freely from our tongues. And then only will we at last be free from the dense cloud called illusion and will we be able to see your divine lotus feet eternally.

Bhakti Caitanya Svāmī / Bhakti-caru Svāmī / Rasānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Lakṣmaṇeśvara dāsa brahmacārī / Dvārakeśvara dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Amalendra dāsa Bhakta Svapana dāsa / Bhakta Gokula dāsa / Bhakta Tapodhama dāsa / Bhakta Satpāla dāsa R.K. Gupta & family / L.S. Thakur / R.C. Kaushal / P.L. Sathi / Krishan Kumar

## Chicago

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Your lotus feet are the greatest treasure within the three worlds. Completely aloof from the modes of material nature, you appeared in this world to establish the teachings of Vyāsadeva. From your childhood, Your Grace worshiped Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, organized Ratha-yātrā festivals, and held *kīrtana.* Never forgetting Kṛṣṇa for a moment, you stayed in India for sixty-nine years, and then under the instruction of Śrī Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Ṭhākura, you appeared in the West, like the early morning rising sun.

Just as Kṛṣṇa blessed this planet five thousand years ago with the imprints of His lotus feet, so you also placed your lotus feet on the land across the ocean that nonpreachers dare not cross. From Butler, Pennsylvania, to New York to Los Angeles and around the world, you benedicted all the unfortunate souls of Kali-yuga. Your mercy knows no bounds, as you magnanimously give shelter to anyone who comes your way. Delivering devotional service and love of God, you are a pure ambassador of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, your accomplishments are endless and unfathomable; how can we describe them? If one had thousands of mouths, then he could chant your glories for thousands of *kalpas* and never come to an end. If one had thousands of ears they would not be enough to hear your unique pastimes of delivering the fallen conditioned souls. We stand in awe of your greatness, and with all humility at our command, we pray to you, please hear us.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if ever by our carelessness we commit some error in our service, please correct us and forgive our faults. If somehow we become offensive in our attitude please destroy the seeds of envy within our hearts. If by some chance we swerve from the path of *bhakti,* please set us straight and rectify our faults. And if somehow or other we forget Kṛṣṇa, please remind us of His lotus feet. Please give us the strength to push on the *saṅkīrtana* movement, and free us from all material desires so that we may work cooperatively for this goal. Please protect us from the poison of lusty thoughts, and when we leave this world, please assist us to remember Kṛṣṇa's holy name. And one more thing, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, when we have finished our service in this world, please let us accompany you to some other place. Please let us be with you while you vanquish the atheists, and sit by you as you lead ecstatic *kīrtana.*

We are all your children, and we are helpless without you. We need your direction at every step. Whatever service we render is meant just to please you. Please protect us and give us the shelter of your lotus feet.
