## Cleveland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism."**

Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto my most beloved spiritual master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, who has so kindly opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge.

As explained by Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, "The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can attain pure devotional service. I bow to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this awe is immediate when one reflects on your transcendental activities in service to the *guru-paramparā* system, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Simply by remaining surrendered at the lotus feet of your spiritual master and always remembering and serving Kṛṣṇa with love and devotion, you created the Vrndavana atmosphere wherever you went.

*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* explains the real meaning of "holy place." A holy place is where the Supreme Personality of Godhead is glorified by His pure devotees. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have spread the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, as well as the entire disciplic succession, all over the universe. This is the most wonderful achievement. To do this, one must be directly empowered by Kṛṣṇa.

It is not possible to properly glorify your wonderful activities, for we feel like illiterate fools attempting to do so. But reflecting on a few, we can always remember how in the morning, before *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, you would sit on your beautiful *vyāsāsana* with your beautiful *karatālas* or gong, and your head would rock so nicely, and all the devotees would feel great transcendental pleasure as you chanted *Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava.* By your being absorbed in thought of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa would be glorified by your most sweet, transcendentally pleasing voice just like a flowing river of nectar.

Also, we remember the days when you would challenge the scientists, the *yogīs, bhogīs, rogīs,* and so on. Sometimes the devotees would write you letters, and you would always reply by saying, "I have received your letter and carefully noted the contents..." and you would give some transcendental encouragement, then would close, saying, "Hoping this meets you in the best of health and blissful Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your ever well-wisher..."

So many things we remember, but foremost we understand that you have given us your instructions. They are in our hearts and in every page and every word of your books. So we will try to execute them by endeavoring with firm faith and determination to spread the glories of the Śrī Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana* movement. We will always worship, study, praise, distribute, glorify, and honor your books, and in this way we will never be separated from your divine self.

*Your humble servants,*  
The devotees of ISKCON Cleveland

## Columbus

Let us offer our most respectful prostrated obeisances unto the lotuslike feet of our most munificent benefactor, Your Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, the bona fide representative of ,the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Hari, Kṛṣṇa!

Dear Gurudeva, most worshipable *guru,* we beg you to accept this collection of words of feeble praise as our Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you as a token gesture of appreciation to you for engaging us in Kṛṣṇa's service this year. We pray that you will continue to allow us the privilege of serving you life after life, eternally.

Your glory, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, easily surpasses the excrutiating brilliance of the light of one hundred million suns rising simultaneously. In your fathomless mercy you are accustomed to accept such puny candlelightlike offerings of praise that we are able to eke forth from our hearts darkened by envy of God.

O superexcellent teacher, you have come here to purify our consciousness so we can see the perils that await those who seek permanent refuge in earthly pleasure. As tiny seafaring vessels are mercilessly smashed and crushed by the raging fury of a midwinter storm, our broken dreams of selfish carnal delights are repeatedly washed up in splinters on the beach of despair by the waves of time.

Dear savior, you have come here, forsaking personal bodily comfort, to rescue us lost and fallen children from the holocaust of lust, anger, and greed that subtly permeates every corner of the material world. You have boldly defied the searing heat of the flames of sinful desire that are roaring on every side.

Most sublimely peaceful servant of Kṛṣṇa, you have shown us why we are toiling mindlessly, nation again nation, brother against brother, father against son, man against wife, virtually eating each other alive. We have become maddened by sinful habits. In this atmosphere, even the most abominable activities are commonly accepted as normal and healthy. In every marketplace naked flesh is sold or used to sell roasted flesh or intoxicants neatly packaged in clear plastic cellophane.

Dear guiding light, you are dispensing knowledge of pure love of Kṛṣṇa By your direction the spiritual world is unfolding before us. You have given our tired eyes Kṛṣṇa in His beautiful Deity forms, enabling us to decorate Him with beautiful colored flowers, jewels, and brightly ornamented cloth. You have introduced us to the sumptuous taste of unlimitedly variegated and ever-increasingly attractive *kṛṣṇa-prasāda* to tickle our bored tongues with spiritual delight. You have anointed our parched ears with the sweet, soothing balm of the nectarean melodies of unending songs of praise of our transcendental Lord. You have enticed our stagnant minds with the most relishable philosophy and stimulating stories from the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* You have seduced our noses with the fragrance of forest flowers and incense offered first for Kṛṣṇa's pleasure. You have challenged our intellect with the task of redeeming the universe. You have gathered us together and given us the companionship of your saintly disciples. You've taught us how to avoid sinful activities, and you've relieved our hearts of their burden of guilt. You've led us dancing into the streets of the world. You have transformed our lives into an uninterrupted festival. No one can deny all this.

You're showing us that Kṛṣṇa was here all along, right before us, and as you teach us to serve Him, we can see Him too. Though the darkness behind us haunts us and beckons to us, you have given us a higher taste. We cannot go back now. Though we are not yet pure we can understand by seeing you that ever-increasing perfection is possible. We accept your guiding hand.

On every page of this book, dear worshipable master, the glories of your accomplishments are sung. Repeatedly, from all corners of the globe, comes the painful cry that we cannot repay you though we long to with all our hearts. On this wonderful day honoring your appearance here, this wonderful, wonderful day, we are holding festivals in your honor all over the world. There will be dancing and chanting and feasting like the world has never seen before, because with each passing day we become more and more appreciative of what you are doing to our lives. Everyone will be attracted to your lotus feet, and through you approach Kṛṣṇa.

We who are taking shelter here at New Bahulaban would like to especially thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the association of your most beloved disciple, Śrīla Kīrtanānanda Mahārāja. His unalloyed devotion to you has helped us to appreciate your greatness.

Lord Brahma once prayed to Kṛṣṇa to take birth as a humble blade of grass on the outskirts of Vṛndāvana. He was indicating that he saw that the residents of Vṛndāvana are such exalted personalities because of their pure and confidential service to Kṛṣṇa. He considered it an honor to be graced with the dust of their feet on his head. Similarly, those of us at your New Bahulaban temple in Columbus, Ohio, feel very fortunate to be situated as the proverbial "back lawn" of New Vrindaban. In this capacity we understand that our only avenue of approaching you is as the servants of the most humble servants of your servant.

## Cuzco

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Through my contaminated eyes I contemplate the heartrending landscape of this material world, Dantesque spectacle, the result of our activities from millions of past births, in which we have become shipwrecked due to our insatiable lust. It has been pleasantly accommodated in the residence of the mind, which is so vulnerable and unhappy because it accepted the company of someone who apparently seemed to be your friend, but who in reality has only caused more and more suffering from time immemorial.

Kṛṣṇa says,

*āvṛtaṁ jñānam etena jñānino nitya-vairiṇā*  
*kāma-rūpeṇa kaunteya duṣpūreṇānalena ca*

**"The pure consciousness of the living entity is covered by his eternal enemy in the form of lust, which is never satisfied and burns like fire."**

O Ācāryadeva! In the middle of such disaster and darkness emerges the illuminated ship of your lotus feet, dissipating the terrible shadows of ignorance, pushing aside the waves of suffering which carry the soul from one place to another in *saṁsāra,* the material existence.

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"The spiritual master receives blessings from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours rain over a forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master saves the materially afflicted world, extinguishing the burning fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances to the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is an ocean of auspicious qualities."**

So, because you are an ocean of auspicious qualities, you can put an end to the inauspicious ocean of *māyā* in which we are helplessly drifting. You are the destroyer of the lust which breaks our unfaithful hearts–so unfaithful that even after hearing so much preaching and nectarean instructions, we continue contemplating the betraying eyes of *māyā,* hallucinated by her bewitching spell.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! You know very well that I'm incapable of following your instructions and that my mind is completely offensive and full of lust, and that also my birth is lower than *mleccha.* But still you are giving me so many opportunities to advance spiritually that for me it is impossible to understand.

On this occasion we remember your divine appearance on this planet. Now you have left, but also remain. You sent us your dear devotee, Śrīla Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami Mahārāja, so we could understand the exalted position of the *guru.* You remain in the form of the *mahā-mercy* of your eleven most perfect disciples, the *ācāryas* of ISKCON, who continue your task of distributing the *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to rescue millions of Rākṣasas and *mlecchas.* Here in Cuzco Mandir this is being verified practically. It is said that this city of Cuzco was the demoniac center where the armies of Rāvaṇa took shelter after being vanquished by Lord Rāmacandra, and that many ghostly entities live here. But after all, surprisingly, after the visit of Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Mahārāja to this temple, hundreds of persons are now chanting the *mahā-mantra* and taking *prasāda* every week, and the number of *bhaktas* is increasing considerably every month.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Jaya Patita-pāvana!  
All glories to you, servant of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī.

Your mercy is expanding to everyone, even to the lowest persons.

So since we are the lowest, we beg: please have mercy upon us and accept this small offering, and bless us with your divine service.

*Your most contaminated servants of Cuzco Mandira,*

## Dallas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*1. Through the transcendental chain of command*  
*The message of Mahāprabhu came into your hands,*  
*You carried it carefully for so many years*  
*Never faltering once, despite others' fears.*

*2. You conceived of a world of pure devotees–*  
*Vaikuṇṭha angels with tilaka and beadbags, sārīs and dhotīs.*  
*Who would have thought it possible in this hell–*  
*A jungle where only dangerous animals dwell.*

*3. The seed that was planted by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Ṭhākura*  
*Became a full-blown tree of love of God by your valor*  
*Coming all alone to the land of the barbarians,*  
*Turning the meat-eaters into kṛṣṇa-prasāda vegetarians.*

*4. And the more we relish the nectarean fruit of this tree*  
*Through chanting, dancing, feasting, and worshiping the Deity*  
*The more our desire grows to distribute this benediction*  
*And thus it is you who are fulfilling Lord Caitanya's prediction.*

*5. That the holy names will spread all over the world*  
*It is all by your preaching so pure, forceful, and bold.*  
*Even today we strongly feel your protection and guidance*  
*In the form of your Deity, books, and devotees–we are always in your presence.*

*6. O master of masters, kindly bestow on us your grace*  
*The knowledge of Kṛṣṇa that leads us far from this place*  
*Of birth, death, disease, and old age–how unsafe!*  
*To the eternal atmosphere of knowledge, bliss, and full life.*

*7. And in that fateful last year as you brought us all closer*  
*So intense feelings of separation would protect us in the future.*  
*You skillfully transferred your great burden of love*  
*Onto the shoulders of those whom Māyā can't move.*

*8. So all of your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālacandajī Mandira*  
*Do offer their deepest devotions at your lotus feet year after year,*  
*And as your transcendental family expands through the ages*  
*We will forever glorify Your Divine Grace as the greatest of all sages.*

## Delhi

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept the New Delhi temple devotees' most worthless obeisances at your lotus feet.

All that you spoke throughout your lifetime is significant and full of meaning. We very clearly remember how once you said that our love for you would be seen by observing how much we cooperate with each other after you leave. On November 14,1977, you left for joining in the pastimes of the flute player, Kṛṣṇa, and since that day we have been on trial. We are all fallen, and hence are trying to be the independent masters of all that we survey. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are really hard nuts to crack. Please give us a kick in the back. We fools deserve it.

Once, a dying father called in his sons and staged a small demonstration. The father picked up a single stick and broke it very easily. Then he took two sticks and broke them, too. Compared to the first attempt, he had to use more energy in breaking two sticks. Then he tried three sticks together, which was still harder than before. Like this, he ultimately picked up a big bunch of sticks and tried his best to break it. But he failed. *Māyā,* the external energy of the Lord, is strong, and she always tries her best to spoil the individual's devotion for the Lord. But if the individuals get together, by wholeheartedly keeping Kṛṣṇa at the center they can withstand even the strongest onslaughts of *māyā.* In the spiritual world, this mentality is the norm. Every single citizen of the spiritual kingdom of the Lord prefers the only common center, and that is the cool shade of the lotus feet of the Lord.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, innumerable times you have reminded us that everyone in the material world is the enemy of Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. So the solution is that ISKCON devotees must get together. United we will stand and expand; divided we will scatter and fall. Push on this movement, that is your instruction, so let us follow that sincerely. The cooperative devotional spirit of all of your disciples is what makes this movement. So cooperation is the basic, essential thing.

Actually, you have challenged all of us by uttering the statement that you are going to watch our behavior after you are no longer physically present with us. So every one of us who claims to have some love for you must have this cooperative spirit. Falling at your feet, we humbly beg again and again, O beloved spiritual master, that could you please be merciful enough to instill that spirit of cooperation in the core of our hearts, so that there may be some meaning to our existence. Otherwise we remain,

*Your useless servants,*

## Denver

***"The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on the forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master extinguishes the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my most humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master."***

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You are the mercy incarnation of Kṛṣṇa. You are compared to a rain cloud, which pours rain freely everywhere. By your causeless mercy, the preaching work of your disciples has become successful in every corner of the world. In this way your mercy has become available to everyone.

By this mercy one can perceive the burning desire in the hearts of the fallen souls to enjoy separately from Kṛṣṇa. The living entity is described as being an animal lost in a vast desert. The animal is always seeing a mirage, thinking it to be water. The animal continues like this over and over again, until finally it meets death. This is the position of the lost conditioned souls within this material world. They are always hoping and searching for some relief from the miseries of material existence. By your causeless mercy you have given one the opportunity to cross over this desert of material existence by distributing profusely this knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By giving this knowledge of the all-attractive Supreme Lord and His eternal abode, you are convincing conditioned living entities to dovetail their sinful desires into pure desires to serve Kṛṣṇa and to return to their rightful place in the eternal abode of the Supreme Lord.

One can actually experience the spiritual world simply by visiting one of your beautiful temples, which you singlehandedly established all over the world. In these temples, the lost conditioned souls have the opportunity to join Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement, which consists of chanting, dancing, feasting, and preaching the sublime philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

The order of Lord Caitanya is to spread this movement to every town and village, and you have followed the instructions perfectly. Although we are very fallen, you have allowed us to assist you in your mission to save the world. We have nothing to offer you in return, because everything we have, you have given us. We only hope that you will kindly accept our bodies, minds, and words in pushing on this *saṅkīrtana* movement. This is your causeless mercy and our only qualification for understanding the sublime message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so kind that you are allowing us to profusely distribute your books around the world, even though we come from lowborn families. By this activity, so many of us are feeling the highest' ecstasy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Also, you are so magnanimous because you allow us to take credit for our meager endeavor.

It says in *śāstra,*

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"By the mercy of the spiritual master, one is benedicted with the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, no one can make any spiritual advancement. At least three times a day I should offer my most respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of my spiritual master."**

We implore you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please engage us in this *saṅkīrtana* movement eternally, to help assist you in fulfilling the desire of your spiritual master. Please give us the strength and the heart to carry out your mission to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village.

## Detroit

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, I bow down with great awe and reverence at your lotus feet because you have forced open my eyes with the torch of knowledge and are pulling me out from the darkness of deepest ignorance.

O Gurudeva, how is it possible to even begin to offer my suitable appreciation to Your Divine Grace? Even Lord Kṛṣṇa is feeling inadequate in trying to repay you for your most loving and confidential service to Him. Therefore, what can a wretched and sinful person of this material world say to you?

O spiritual master, it is certainly true that a teacher can be best judged by his disciples. Therefore, to begin to understand you, one only has to approach your sincere disciples in a humble state of mind to find oneself in the midst of the most exalted pure devotees of the Supreme Lord who are radiating all sublime Vaiṣṇava qualities.

O Vaiṣṇava *ācārya,* this is your real glory. You have come from Goloka Vṛndāvana and created your own Goloka right in the midst of this degraded age of quarrel and hypocrisy, a Goloka Vṛndāvana where pure devotees of the Lord are always engaged in hearing and chanting with love about the glories of the Supreme Lord and His devotees.

O representative of Vyāsadeva, the best way to increase your glories is that we make your instructions our life and soul, that we become pure devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. If we want you to be truly glorified all over the world, then it is our unavoidable duty to become one-hundred-percent pure.

O dearmost servant of Kṛṣṇa, in this way your potency, your love and compassion and your instructions will flood the world, and all the lost souls will make great strides toward returning back to home, back to Kṛṣṇa.

O lord and master, there is but one submission. Please don't let this servant of yours fall away from the shelter of your lotus feet. I am low, fallen, devoid of all strength and intelligence. When I examine myself, I find nothing of value.

O Gurudeva, your mercy is therefore essential to me. If you are not merciful, I will constantly weep and there will be no value to my life. Please do not let me become useless.

*Your eternal servants at Devasādhana Mandira,*

## Dublin

O lord, we know you love to preach;  
The lowest of the low,  
The foolish, and the fallen lift  
And on their heads your gift of love bestow.

O lord, we know you love to teach,  
And everywhere you go  
You pull the sinful souls from hell  
And save them from an endless well of woe.

O lord, we love to hear your speech.  
Your words of wisdom flow  
Like streams of nectar from your lips,  
And the way from māyā's grips do show.

O lord, we really want to reach  
That stage and stoutly grow  
In love and service to your feet,  
So those we meet, your love through us may know.

## Ecuador

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. Those lotus feet are our only shelter within the three worlds. We can only hope to wash them with the sincere desire to serve you. That is the only offering we can render. And if our desire is truly sincere, Lord Caitanya will be pleased, and we can collect the torrential rainfall of mercy from your feet and wash away the unwanted things in our hearts.

Our hearts can be purified only by following this simple, sublime process of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, which you have taught us. You have told us many times the process is simple, but it is our stone-cold hearts that cannot perceive this sublime message. Only the fire of your preaching can burn away these obstacles. Yes, you have left us physically, but you have left a storehouse of transcendental knowledge in your books. That is our protection.

Prabhupāda, you are the expert surgeon. You can cut away all of the illusion covering our darkened vision with your instructions. But we must succumb to the surgery. We must reflect our love for you in our everyday dealings with our Godbrothers. By our service to them we can measure our desire to serve you. We must also accept your surgical instruments, your books, and then the operation will be successful. All instructions are awaiting us in those transcendental books. If we can cooperate nicely, we can operate on the entire society and bring this dead body to life. That is the perfect example which you gave us. But that means preaching and profusely distributing the unlimited ocean of bliss which you have given us.

Prabhupāda, you are always full of compassion for the fallen conditioned souls. We are the most fallen and need your mercy constantly. But now that we are beginning to taste a little nectar from following your perfect instructions, we must follow your example. You are always preaching. While you were here in your physical form you never stopped preaching. Now that you have left, you are turning the tide of history. Your books are effulgent jewels, spreading knowledge in all directions. Now we must be transcendental mirrors and focus your mercy on the hearts of all living beings. You are the perfect example, never stopping for one second. You are Jagad-guru. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds. You have brought the eternal religious principles within the reach of everyone. You have set the pace for the spiritual advancement of the entire universe, and we are simply running behind you with lead weights on our feet. We can never catch up. We can never repay you. We can only hope to serve you eternally. In this regard, Prahlāda Mahārāja said, *so 'haṁ kathaṁ nu visṛje tava bhṛtya-sevām:* "How can I leave the service of my spiritual master, who has brought me to the transcendental position of direct association with the Lord? My duty is to serve him." The only way we can lighten our load is by serving you. We are crying out from behind for your causeless mercy, birth after birth.

*Your eternal, insignificant servants,*

Guru-prasāda dāsa / Mṛganātha dāsa / Lokabhāvana dāsa / Gopīkānta dāsa / Bhakta Joselito Bhakta Jorge / Mādhavī-devī dāsī

## FATE

Master of truth, master of light  
Give of love, giver of sight  
Vision of peace, wielder of might  
Bless us this day, brighten our night.

Tormented hearts, creatures of dark  
Weeping in loneliness, extinguished sparks  
Cry for relief from torture and grief  
Not obtaining salvation they drown in their tears.

Still the roar of steel cannot conceal  
The rainbow of bliss eternal and real  
When there's a mirror as bright as your heart  
Blazing with mercy, gleaming with love.

The shackles of darkness fall like child's toys  
The word raises arms to you in speechless joy  
While towering high above ruins of time  
You gaze full of mercy upon us who're blind.

Who turn their palefaces disfigured by fear  
Sensing your wonderful shining is near  
Regaining our virtue and the power to see  
We bless this day ever for giving us thee.

## Fiji

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Our most beloved Gurudeva,*

Please accept our humble daṇḍavat-praṇāmas at your lotus feet again and again on the auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā.

We were such fallen souls, but by your causeless mercy you gave us shelter at your lotus feet, accepted us as your disciples, and engaged us in the devotional service of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Kāliya. We are really very proud to be your disciples.

Right from the very beginning, from our first meeting with you at Nadi Airport, our greatest pleasure was to serve you. The first instruction you gave us was to build a nice temple in Fiji. From that day our only wish was to fulfill your desire, and by the power of your words that desire was fulfilled. Śrīla Prabhupāda! It was only by your mercy that such a nice temple was built. Now many fallen souls have a chance to know about Kṛṣṇa and to serve Him and chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra.*

Dear Gurudeva, we are remembering you at every moment. Twice you came to Fiji, but because of some inconvenience you could not stay at our house, so we built a new house–"Prabhupāda Nivāsa"–and thought now we would be able to serve you very nicely. But we were so unfortunate that you could not come. Your separation is very painful for us. Whenever we think of your physical separation, it seems that someone is piercing our hearts with a spear, and this makes us cry,

Another wonderful thing you have done is that you have selected your foremost disciples and empowered them to be spiritual masters who can guide us as you were guiding. Now there is another chance for us to serve your representatives Śrīla Rāmeśvara Swami and Śrīla Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami in your place. In that way we get the satisfaction of serving you. We will never forget the day we came to meet you at Māyāpur in 1976. That was a real pilgrimage!

O Gurudeva! Please give us shelter at your lotus feet, and be merciful on us so that we can get knowledge and strength to push on your mission in Fiji with the help of your present *ācāryas* Śrīla Rāmeśvara Swami and Śrīla Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami Mahārāja.

Jaya Gurudeva.

*Your eternal servants,*  
The devotees of ISKCON Fiji

## France

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the world garlands you, world *ācārya,* with scented words of gratitude. Though the praise flows like an endless river, no one can capture by his thoughts, words, or feelings the extent of gratitude owed you for your incomparable work on behalf of the conditioned souls of this world.

We offer repeated obeisances to the memory of you, which lingers in this world like the magic smell of some exotic flower. Its essence permeates our every sound, sight, taste, smell, and touch. It colors and transforms our whole perception of the world about us. It crystallizes our understanding and thus makes Kṛṣṇa consciousness an irrefutable reality. Being sons of Kali, we choose to turn our backs to that evidence and entertain a vision of illusion as suits our perverted desire. But we do so with a heavy heart, knowing that we are wrong, and longing to respond to your poem about the spiritual world.

By the will of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Lord, time inexorably grinds the future in an endless pursuit of the Lord's inconceivable plan. Life and things are caught in the meshes of destiny. The struggle to free oneself from its afflictions but nourishes its binding force. There is no escape. As waves on the ocean of eternity, years turn to centuries, and centuries into eons. Throughout, the living entity transforms itself into countless varieties of bodies that spell different shades of pain. Through his mutations he keeps intact only a faint, fleeting hope that lands nowhere, like some soft bird flying into unlimited space. You have come to give this seed a landing place, where it may sprout into devotional service.

Once upon a time, in the dark age of Kali, in the twenty-eighth *divya-yuga* of the Vaivasvata Manu, the population of the planet earth suffered greatly under the illusion of a material concept of life. The entire population had lost contact with the spiritual dimension of life. With hearts seeped in despair, hopeless, they sought frantically the favors of transitory happiness. And Maheśvara, the Supreme Lord, displaying again His inconceivable mercy, appeared as one of them and showed them the path of liberation–the congregational chanting of the *mahā-mantra:*

Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu's appearance is like that of the sun, piercing faintly at first on the horizon and gradually flooding with light the entire hemisphere. We, as disciples of *nitya-līlā-praviṣṭa* His Divine Grace Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, take full shelter at his feet, recognizing him as the full and complete representative of the Lord for this age.

Devotional service is like a great science for the respiritualization of mankind, and the *saṅkīrtana* movement is the very heart of this science. Many great *ācāryas* have explored different aspects of devotional life as proposed by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrīla Prabhupāda, by his unalloyed and selfless dedication, became full and sole heir of their great discoveries. Their vision he took within his heart and amplified it until it reached the outermost limits of the universe. It was he who, endowed with inconceivable potency, reversed the flood of Kali. It was he who, with inconceivable wisdom, set the course for humanity. It was he who, with unfathomable mercy, made devotional service accessible to our childlike grasp. It was he, only, who made devotional service available to the millions. He is truly the savior of the world, and because his lotus heart never strays from the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, he is to be worshiped as being as good as Kṛṣṇa Himself.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, small is our mind; we are the child of lust; weak is our determination. Forgetful of the vision you wanted to imprint within us, we falter in our faith and are overcome by the influence of material habits. As we struggle from day to day, concerned with the things of life, we feel totally ineffectual in carrying out your instructions. We barely maintain our senses and are ashamed of the shambles within our minds. Devoid of any spiritual depth or potency, we hold to one single-minded conclusion, one article of faith: as the sun has light, so you carry incomprehensible mercy. Let us abide by the shelter of your lotus feet, although we do not merit such consideration.

*Your most unworthy servants,*

## Gainesville

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Another year has passed now since our last offering to you, and we are truly amazed at how everything is continuing so nicely by your mercy.

Sometimes we feel the pain of your absence, wondering how we can carry on without your strength and guidance–then at the next moment we can feel your presence and see how wonderful it is that you have organized everything so intelligently that even bumbling fools like ourselves can attempt to carry out the mission of Lord Caitanya.

We can see the product of your endeavors in your appointed *gurus* and advanced devotees guiding this movement. We can see how, simply by remembering and serving you, they are developing many of your qualities.

Anytime we start feeling the burden of trying to push on Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement and wondering if it is worth the difficulty, we simply have to think of your determination.

Kṛṣṇa states in *Bhagavad-gītā, vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana:* "Those who are on this path are resolute in purpose and their aim is one." You have shown the greatest determination in your untiring effort to execute the desire of your spiritual master. So when we think of the difficulties you tolerated on our behalf, we feel more capable of carrying on your movement.

We were all rushing madly to the hellish regions by the grace of Kali-yuga, and if not for you, there is no telling where we would be now and what condition the world would be in.

So we are thanking you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bestowing your unlimited mercy on us, and we are praying that Kṛṣṇa will always allow us to remember your instructions and your lotus feet.

*Your fallen servants in Gainesville, Florida*

## Germany

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*sri caitanya-mano-'bhiṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

*vande ’haṁ śrī-guroḥ śrī-yuta-pada-kamalaṁ śrī-gurūn vaiṣṇavāṁś ca*  
*śrī-rūpaṁ sāgrajātaṁ saha-gaṇa-raghunāthānvitam taṁ sa-jīvam*  
*ādvaitaṁ sāvadhūtam parijana-sahitaṁ kṛṣṇa-caitanya-devaṁ*  
*śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-pādān saha-gaṇa-lalitā-śrī-viśākhānvitāṁś ca*

*nama om visnu-pdddya kṛṣṇa-presthdya bhu-tale*  
*srimate bhaktiveddnta-svdmin iti ndmine*

*namas te sdrasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracdrine*  
*nirvisesa-sunyavddi-pdscdtya-desa-tdrine*

**Save Us, Śrīla Prabhupāda**

This day you've appeared with Caitanya's full might  
To open our eyes to the horrible sight  
Of the lust and the envy which we have embraced  
And the cycle of births in which our soul wastes,

Of the lust and the envy which we have embraced  
And the cycle of births in which our soul wastes,

O save us, dear master, from karma's onslaught–  
The hellish reactions for the sins we have wrought–  
Over millions of years in myriad forms  
We have died without shelter, in darkness forlorn.

The eating of meat, and gambling and sex,  
And intoxicants' use serve as māyā's cruel hex  
Which bind us to ignorance stretching over all time–  
Please save us from this, O master sublime!

**Teach Us, Śrīla Prabhupāda**

In Bhagavad-gītā Lord Kṛṣṇa has told  
The soul is eternal and shining like gold,  
Giving life to these bodies, which we change like dress  
This spiritual knowledge upon us please bless!  
For one so enlightened has no need to fear,  
There's no worldly claims to which he holds dear.  
Such a pure soul can then fix his mind  
In service to Kṛṣṇa, this world's rarest find.

By hearing and chanting and remembering the Lord  
And obeying His will, we'll then get the sword  
To cut ourselves free from this hellish place–  
It’s only achieved by your divine grace.  
Help Us, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your grace is not captured by envious greed–  
It's meant to be given to all souls in need.  
So when we receive it, there's created a debt–  
We repay you by helping others out of the net.  
Your mission to spread all over the earth:  
"Give all men the chance for which human life's worth!  
Whether it's taken as mantra, prasāda, or book  
The mercy's the same, let them come have a look!"

Now we as your servants must carry this weight  
Of delivering the fallen from māyā's cruel fate.  
Oh, help us this day, Lord Kṛṣṇa's dear friend,  
To fulfill your purpose even past this life's end!

*–Your disciples of ISKCON Germany*

## Gītā-Nāgarī

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this appearance day of Your Divine Grace, please let me thank you for pulling me from the ocean of nescience. You are my lifeline. You are everything to me. You are the object of my attachment for spiritual life; your pastimes have captured my heart. Hearing of your words and activities is by far the most relishable thing I know. For me it is sufficient to hear about Your Divine Grace. You are the person *bhāgavatam.* Your activities are perfect and complete. If I can understand your activities, I will be complete in knowledge. Your transcendental presence is very wonderful, always offering guidance and inspiration at every moment. You are like a great rain cloud spreading your mercy everywhere, without hesitation, full of compassion. At great personal risk, you have accepted me as your disciple. How can I ever repay you? You spent your life doing the work of your beloved spiritual master, so I will spend the rest of my life doing your work, hoping that you will become pleased.

*Your servants,*

## Greece

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Many people in this age say that spiritual life is a nice ideal but impossible. With your example, you convinced everyone that God realization is not just a theory but a beautiful, factual attainment.

By your mercy, the nectarean realm of Lord Caitanya's teachings is now available. There is no other way to overcome Kali-yuga and kill the demon of sex-life.

You know how to please Kṛṣṇa, and you teach us the art. Every movement or word of yours reveals transcendental qualities that attract even Kṛṣṇa. Your association is always fresh, more and more desirable.

You attracted us and you made us attached to you, and in this way you saved our lives. We have an eternal debt to repay to you. To have you we must please you, and your most cherished desire is to save the conditioned souls. So we dedicated our lives to assist you in pleasing Kṛṣṇa by this *saṅkīrtana-rasa.*

Please allow us to represent you nicely and serve you this way. All glories to Your Divine Grace and the devotees that please you.

Ayodhyāpati dāsa adhikārī / Pañcajanya dāsa adhikārī / Citraka dāsa brahmacārī / Nanda-gopa dāsa brahmacārī / Śrīkārī-devī dasī

## Guatemala

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

It has been simply because of your merciful desire that in only thirteen years, thousands of Westerners have become interested to know about the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

We were born in families of *mlecchas* and *yavanas,* and ?now, without any shelter in this material world, we are ' praying for your compassionate glance. You taught us how to obtain life's ultimate goal, and although sometimes we wanted to close our eyes to this truth, you helped us along, just like a loving father. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the enthusiasm to try and please you, for without pleasing you, what is the use of our maintaining our lives?

You are devotional service personified. We pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa to let us always appreciate Your Divine Grace, and even though, because of our innumerable material desires, we don't have the strength, we beg that you take us, just as the breeze carries the raindrops to their destination, to the lotus feet of Govinda, and engage us in His loving devotional service. *Prema-bhakti yaha hoite, avidyā vināśa yāte.* It is only because of your presence that we have remembered our eternal obligation to return home, back to Godhead. Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Being completely useless, we beg you that birth after birth you give us the shelter of your wonderful lotus feet.

*Your most fallen servants at Guatemala Mandira,*

## Guyana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,* Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

After transmigrating through millions upon millions of births in higher and lower species of life forms, by the grace of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa we have been given this rare and precious opportunity to be accepted as your disciples and servants. We are not even intelligent enough to fully appreciate and make use of this great boon. But although we keep committing one offense after another by not strictly following the instructions of Your Divine Grace, still you are always so kind and forgiving that you do not deprive us of your association, nor do you ever deny us the opportunity of advancing in spiritual life by rendering loving devotional service at your lotus feet. How else can we get the mercy of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa but by pleasing you, His dearmost confidential servitor? As you have so often told us, one can get the favor of a very wealthy man simply by offering a sweetmeat worth two cents to his little son. When the child becomes pleased by this insignificant gift, automatically the wealthy father becomes pleased with the donor. In the same way, we are able to get Kṛṣṇa's mercy by obtaining your favor through our insignificant service. Thus we are able to advance on the path back home, back to Godhead.

Our restless and fickle minds, always wandering in various directions seeking after sense gratification, can be controlled by remembering and endeavoring with patience and determination to follow your instructions. These instructions, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are like the sun, and they can dissipate the darkness of illusion due to *māyā.* We fervently pray to you to be always engaged as menial servants at your lotus feet.

*Your disciples and granddisciples of Guyana,*

Bhūtādi dāsa / Bhakta Prithvi / Bhakta Ramesh / Bhaktin Vijay / Bhaktin Clevin

## Hong Kong

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
When the caraṇāmṛta flood appeared to recede  
Limited minds would have us pay no heed,  
For after the flood had purified everything in sight  
Some thought that only Bengal would "suffer the plight."

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
They don't understand about the Lord's flood of mercy  
How it never recedes but goes on expanding endlessly.  
By your divine grace nothing is impossible, but to be more specific,  
A flood is now occurring in the eastern Pacific!

The doorway is called Hong Kong, and the dam's about to burst  
Since the full effect of Patita-pāvana's flood has come here first.  
You're directing this deluge, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so even some  
Red Chinese now see the need  
To spread this flood to the parched land beyond  
and plant your bhakti-latā seed.

Yes, in the fire of your divine compassion do we burn  
To free a billion fettered souls–oh, how they yearn  
To regain that lost art, that divine ability  
To dance with you and our beloved Lord for all eternity.

*Your eternal servants,*

Jitarati dāsa / Satya-nārāyaṇa dāsa / Yaśomatī-suta dāsa / Jīvanātha dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma dāsa / Jagat-guru dāsa / Bhakta Jason / Bhakta Ramon / Bhakta Dipak / Bhakta Gulab / Bhakta Abi / Bhakta John / Jānakī-devī dāsī / Jaya Rādhe-devī dāsī / Nitya-līlā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Janis

## Honolulu

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, the dearmost disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You are the topmost swanlike devotee and wandering mendicant. You have traveled all over the entire world spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You personally have inundated the entire world with *kṛṣṇa-prema.* You have destroyed impersonalism and voidism, and you have delivered the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. You are an ocean of all auspicious qualities, and the Vedic scriptures sing of your character. Just like Mahārāja Parīkṣit conquered the personality of Kali by drawing his sword and preparing to kill him, you have conquered the nescience and ignorance of us Kali-yuga-ites by drawing your sword, the pen, and translating over seventy volumes of transcendental literatures. Never has the earth seen such a great personality as you. You arrived in America at the advanced age of seventy with only forty rupees, a pair of *karatālas,* and some *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatams.* And it was you alone who created, maintained, managed, and directed the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, and thus conquered the United States of America and subsequently the whole world. You personally traveled to all the continents and sent your transcendental armies to every town and village. Who can begin to describe your unlimited glories? Even Ananta Śeṣa, with His unlimited mouths chanting for unlimited years, could not begin to describe all your glorious qualities and wonderful pastimes. How then can we, who are so ignorant and devoid of any intelligence, give you proper respect? We were all born in *mleccha* and *yavana* families, and at best we could be compared to hogs, dogs, camels, and asses. Never was there a more fallen class of living entities. We were completely sunk in the mire and muck of material existence and encased in decadence and degradation. But you, O venerable Vaiṣṇava, have changed us from monkeys to devotees. You are a touchstone, an ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees. You have accepted us as your disciples. Such a compassionate personality is nowhere to be found in the three worlds. On this most glorious and auspicious day of your divine appearance, we are giving all thanks and praise to you, our beloved spiritual master, and we are begging that somehow or other you become pleased with our insignificant service and thus give us the shade of your lotus feet. We simply want to become pure in heart so that we can follow your instructions to preach and distribute your books, birth after birth. O master, this is the only mercy we are seeking, and if you are not merciful unto us, then we can only weep, and we will not be able to maintain our lives.

*Your eternal servants at New Navadvīpa-dhāma,*

## Houston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day, we cherish the opportunity and privilege of offering our prayers of gratitude to you, our eternal father, guide, and well-wisher. We are still ,your small children, Śrīla Prabhupāda; it has been only a few years since you enabled us to take spiritual birth. What can a young child know about the opulence, activities, and accomplishments of his very great and famous father? Furthermore, being very immature, most of us never had your intimate, personal association and instruction. You are a maha-bhagavata, the greatest devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. By what means can we understand how to serve you?

By your own extraordinary mercy, you have personally revealed your purpose in coming here and accepting us, even though we are so wretched and unqualified to be your children.From the time of your arrival in the West in 1965, your writings have clearly established your life's mission. In the Markine Bhagavata-dharma you pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa,

*tomāra icchāya saba hoy māyā-baśa*  
*tomāra icchāya nāśa māyār parāśa*

*taba icchā hoyjadi tādera uddhār*  
*bujhibe niścai tabe kathā se tomār*

**"All living entities have become under the control of the illusory energy by Your will, and therefore, if You like, by Your will they can also be released from the clutches of illusion. I wish that You may deliver them. Therefore if You so desire their deliverance, then only will they be able to understand Your message."**

*ki ko're bujhābo katāh bara sei cāhi*  
*hṛdoyer abhadra sab ghucibe tāhār*

*athaca enecho prabhu kathā bolibāre*  
*je tomār icchā prabhu koro ei bāre*

*akhila jagat-guru! bacana se āmār*  
*alaṅkṛta koribār khamatā tomār*

*taba kṛpā ha’le mor kathā śuddha habe*  
*śunityā sabāra śoka duḥkha je ghucibe*

**"How will I make them understand this message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness? I am very unfortunate, unqualified, and the most fallen. Therefore I am seeking Your benediction so that I can convince them, for I am powerless to do so on my own. Somehow or other, O Lord, You have brought me here to speak about You. Now, my Lord, it is up to You to make me a success or failure as You like. O spiritual master of all the worlds! I can simply repeat Your message, so if You like You can make my power of speaking suitable for their understanding. Only by Your causeless mercy will my words become pure. I am sure that when this transcendental message penetrates their hearts they will certainly feel engladdened and thus become liberated from all unhappy conditions of life."**

In this mood of intense compassion, and with the desire for the upliftment of all conditioned souls, you started ISKCON. Your complete faith in the words of Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master are the strength upon which you preached. There is no doubt that ISKCON is a direct manifestation of Lord Caitanya's mercy. We stand in awe as we behold the vast foundation which you have established, which is actually the framework for the revival of Vedic culture throughout the entire world.

As it is the duty of a good son to carry on the work of the father after the father departs, our fervent prayer to you is to allow us to render some humble service in pushing on Kṛṣṇa consciousness according to your direction. Please purify our hearts and allow us to feel one drop of the compassion for the fallen souls that you have felt. Let us have complete faith in your order and in the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

In your efforts to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you always entreated your Godbrothers to work cooperatively to carry out the order of your Guru Mahārāja. If they did not cooperate with one another, their preaching mission would certainly fail. In the same way, you have warned us that if we quarrel enviously among ourselves, then we shall also fail.

Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī took the blessings and instruction from all the senior Vaiṣṇavas, and thus he was able to enter into full, ecstatic realization of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu's *līlā.* He was fully tasting the nectar of the Lord's inconceivable pastimes, and in a very humble way he described them for the welfare and satisfaction of all living beings. We pray to submit ourselves to the instruction and guidance of your successor *ācāryas* and G.B.C.'s so that we might be confident of properly serving you. As Lord Caitanya's pastimes and desires were revealed through His confidential disciples, let us realize the nectar of your pastimes and instructions through your most intimate and advanced disciples. In this way we may become qualified to preach the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa and to sing of your glories for the purification of the entire world. This will be our greatest pleasure. We are not sincere, neither do we possess any devotion or learning. But we have strong faith in your words, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have allowed us to become your disciples and granddisciples. Now kindly use us to fulfill your plans to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Whatever independence we stubbornly cling to, let it now be cast far away. Please let us remain eternally,

*The humble servants of your servants,*

## International Life Membership Trust

*Guru* means "heavy." Actually, the heaviest thing in this world is *guru.* Everything in the universe, although appearing to be permanent, is eventually swept away by the waves of time and therefore is unfit as a shelter from *māyā.* Herein lies the awesome responsibility of being *guru.*

*pāhi pāhi mahā-yogin deva-deva jagat-pate*  
*nānyaṁ tvad abhayaṁ paśye yatra mṛtyuḥ parasparam*

**"O Lord of lords! Lord of the universe! You are the greatest of mystics! Please protect me, for there is no one else who can save me from the clutches of death in this world of duality."**

In our search for shelter and security, we will find that whomever we approach, except for Kṛṣṇa and His bona fide representative, everyone else is nothing more than an agent for death. Therefore *guru* is not a mundane post, something to be imitated or analyzed by ignorant cheaters. The intimate relationship between the *guru* and the disciples is only revealed to those who enter into it. Subjective outsiders may deride the position of the *guru* by comparing it to a rank in an ecclesiastical hierarchy or to some other exploitative enterprise, but such critics only condemn themselves by committing the *aparādha* known as *maryādā-vyatikrama.* They will ultimately burn to ashes in the fire of their own envy, as in the case of the ancestors of Mahārāja Bhagīratha.

The *guru* appears as a link in the transcendental *sampradāya* in order to deliver the divine sound vibration of the *Vedas* for the easy liberation of the entire world, if only the world is willing to accept.

*sampradāya-vihinā ye mantrās te niṣphalā matāḥ*  
*śrī-brahma-rudra-sanaka-vaiṣṇavā kṣiti-pāvanāḥ*

Within the *Vedas* there are crores of perfected *mantras.* However, outside of the supervision of the *guru* in an actual *sampradāya,* one will only become confused and eventually fall into a dark well full of Māyāvāda philosophies. Under proper direction, the same *mantra* will deliver the highest achievements.

*kibā mantra dila, gosāñi, kibā tāra bala*  
*japite japite mantra karila pāgala*

(Lord Caitanya to His spiritual master Īśvara Purī:) "O my dear lord, what kind of *mantra* have you give Me? I have become mad simply by chanting this *mahā-mantra*!"

The real power of Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, the Jagad-guru, who leads the world *kīrtana* party known as the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, continues to manifest to those who remain and listen to his song.

*Your servants,*

Jalakāra dāsa adhikārī / Śyāma Lāl dāsa brahmacārī / Ekādaśī dāsa brahmacārī / Sītā Ṭhakurāṇī-devī dāsī

## ISKCON Committee for Urban Spiritual Development

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Patita-pāvana hetu tava avatāra.* I was born in the darkest ignorance in one of the lowest bodies in the most fallen conditions. I grew up in the black ghetto, fatherless, surrounded by crime and ignorance. You, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, have taken me in as your son and have opened my eyes with a torchlight of knowledge.

The very first time I heard your voice, I immediately knew that this was not the first time of our meeting, as your voice was very familiar to me. Even then you picked me up out of the most abominable conditions.

The first time I was so fortunate as to have your physical association you were so compassionate to me, allowing me to sit next to you during the *darśana.* At that meeting in New York, you encouraged several devotees and myself to preach in Harlem. Ironically, instead of preaching to the lowest class of men, I went year after year preaching and selling books to scholars all around the world. You, dear Gurudeva, empowered me, despite my uneven education, poor articulation, low birth, etc., to convince and defeat some of the so-called most intellectual men on the planet. You allowed me to train some of your disciples in this service, although they were many times more qualified than myself. Although I have always been so envious and offensive toward your devotees, you have always kept me in the most advanced association. I have no knowledge of Vaiṣṇava etiquette. I have such little love for you and your disciples. I have maintained such superficial determination. I am begging and praying everyday that you forgive me for being the fool I am. I am a disturbance to the Vaiṣṇava community ... I have failed you. Your desire is that all of your disciples become pure devotees. Since I have not attained this status (i.e., become free of material desires), this is a clear indication of my lack of love for you.

I have returned to New York to fulfill your very first request of me. I am dedicating the rest of my useless life to preaching all over the world to the most fallen. I have joined with Brahmānanda Svāmī to assist him with the preaching work in Africa. We have also formed the "ISKCON Committee for Urban Spiritual Development" (U.S.D.). We are taking all of the urban communities in the nation as our field of activities. Already we are having such success as men are coming from all over the movement to join our party. We are contacting celebrities to support our urban spiritual projects. We will visit all of the prisons in the country, preaching to the receptive prisoners, distributing thousands of books. We are establishing very small restaurants in the inner-city communities, allowing ourselves the opportunity to distribute *prasāda* and to preach more intimately. We will visit hundreds of churches and other spiritual groups, sharing Kṛṣṇa consciousness with them. We are having *kīrtana* parties constantly parading through the ghetto streets, and we are making arrangements to have Kṛṣṇa conscious devotees as chaplains at a few schools, as well as teaching courses.

As you have captured me from such a degraded condition, ordered me, encouraged me, and given me such magnanimous blessings, I can never repay you in millions of lifetimes. But I must make some attempt by fully surrendering myself and by helping to bring thousands of others to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

So by your divine arrangement, I am now Bhaktitīrtha Svāmī. Now you must force me to fulfill the purport of this name. Please forgive me, dear father Śrīla Prabhupāda, for not being one of your pure disciples.

Bhaktitīrtha Svāmī / Brahmānanda Svāmī / Jyotsnā dāsa / Viprahita dāsa / Rāghava Caitanya dāsa / Bhūta-bhāvana dāsa / Bhaktagaṇa dāsa / Hari-murāri dāsa / Pāpasthāyī dāsa / Maṇḍana dāsa / Viṣṇumūrti dāsa / Patattrirāja dāsa / Ūrjasvat dāsa / Rasikānanda dāsa / Līlāśuka dāsa / Bhakta Jose / Bhakta Eduardo / Bhakta Swanson / Bhakta Melvin / Bhakta Sonny / Bhakta Tony / Bhakta Raoul / Bhakta Ray / Bhakta Sammy / Śyāmagha-devī dāsī / Vrajamaṇḍala-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Elaine

## Israel

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your having appeared on the Western horizon .was like a comet–a bad omen for the materialistic society: *nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*. This mantra is there, piercing the material covering of our heart–we carry it as our flag; it has become our flag, for each and every one of us.

And where does the West begin? Whatever place a devotee goes, the West is there, as if his shadow; materialistic Western society is everywhere! Mother Earth is calling for help, the globe is in a forest fire, and one can see the dark smoke covering the eyes of those who need so much to see you, to have your association. Who else could, who else can, who else will be able to give those potential devotees light and hope?

Like Lord Boar, you looked into the muddy, maddened crowd to have some rasa in the land of Columbus, that Columbus who missed the Golden Avatāra, being dazzled by the American gold.

But you, you *do not* make mistakes. Unlike the materialistic Columbus, you came to give, not to take! O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, Your Divine Grace carries the Golden Avatāra within your heart. Like a perfect master, you have prepared for the golden opportunity. So perfectly you amalgamated the Golden Avatāra with the American gold, using the *mahā-mantra* 's golden glue for uniting all men under one flag, one *guru*, one religion, one scripture. With so much humor, as if playing, just like Lord Boar you have again picked up the globe, relieved Mother Earth from the heavy burden caused by the "fools and rascals," as you used to say. O Prabhupāda, O Jagad-guru.

*ohe! vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse karuṇā kori'*  
*diyā pada-chāyā, śodha he amāyā,*  
*tomāra caraṇa dhori*

**"O venerable Vaisnava, O ocean of mercy, be merciful unto your servant. Give me the shade of your lotus feet and purify me. I hold on to your lotus feet."**

*chaya bega dami', chaya doṣa śodhi',*  
*chaya guṇa deha' dāse*  
*chaya sat-saṅga, deha' he āmāre*  
*bosechi saṅgera āśe*

**"Teach me to control my six passions, rectify my six faults, bestow upon me the six qualities, and offer unto me the six kinds of holy association."**

*ekākī āmāra, nāhi pāy bolo,*  
*harināma-saṅkīrtane*  
*tumi kṛpā kori', śraddhā-bindu diyā,*  
*deha' kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane*

**"I do not find the strength to carry on alone the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **of the holy name of Hari. Please bless me by giving me just one drop of faith with which to obtain the great treasure of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa."**

*kṛṣṇa se tomāra, kṛṣṇa dite pāra,*  
*tomāra śakati āche*  
*āmi to' kangala, 'kṛṣṇa' 'kṛṣṇa' boll',*  
*dhāi tava pāche pāche*

**"Kṛṣṇa is yours; you have the power to give Him to me. I am simply running behind you, shouting, 'Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa!' "**

Yes, yes, you have Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa is yours! Like madmen we are after you, running, and will run forever and forever, crying, "Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa!"

We have no qualifications whatsoever, and the one we do have–that burning desire to serve Your Divine Grace–is actually not ours but your direct pure, divine mercy upon your children who are born from your divine grace. There is no way by which we can repay you for all that you have given to we who are totally dependent on your mercy and intelligence alone. All we can ask is that you allow your servants to increase their service to your lotuslike feet. This is our only wishful prayer; our wet eyes carry always your beautiful smile. We bow down before Your Divine Grace, our only father and spiritual master, our lord and our very heart and soul.

After the departure of our Śrīla Prabhupāda, may all good fortune and strength be with our most beloved Godbrother and present hope, Śrīla Bhagavān Gosvāmī Mahārāja, by whose divine grace we can go on serving the swanlike personality at whose feet all spiritual masters are taking shelter.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, greatest of all masters, please accept this humble offering from your small and insignificant servants and devotees,

## Italy

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*  
*na hy adbhutam tvac-carandbja-renubhir*  
*hatdmhaso bhaktir adhoksaje 'maid*  
*mauhurtikdd yasya samdgamdc ca me*  
*dustarka-mulo 'pahato 'vivekah*

**"It is not at all wonderful that simply by being covered by the dust of your lotus feet, one immediately attains the platform of pure devotional service to Adhokṣaja, which is not available even to great demigods like Brahma. By associating with you just for a moment, I am now freed from all argument, false prestige, and lack of discrimination, which are the roots of entanglement in the material world. Now I am free from all these problems." (*****Bhāg.*** **5.13.22)**

*Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Even if the spiritual master is not impressed with the qualifications of the disciple, still, today, in our insignificant attempt to express a spark of your splendor, we would like to be masters in the art of poetry.

To glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is easy and difficult at the same time. Who, after seeing you, listening to you, or even only hearing about you, would not have some words of appreciation or admiration? But, on the other hand, how to find proper words to glorify your personality, which the demigods themselves are celebrating on the highest planets? Even if our attempt to offer you some words of glorification will be like the offering of a lamp to the sun or some water to the Ganges, still we are eager to join, on this auspicious day, in the great festival of the chanting of your glories.

Like the *gopīs* who, after the disappearance of Śrī Kṛṣṇa from the *rāsa* dance, found solace imitating the wonderful pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, thus absorbing themselves in His remembrance, so we soothe the pains of separation from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, trying to act as you acted, to speak as you spoke, to think as you thought, permeating our lives with you, like the ether pervades everything. Thus, in every moment, how can we not remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are for us more important than the oxygen we breathe?

By preaching in this material forest, we realize more and more your greatness. You have done the most difficult part of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission. You have cleared the way through this material jungle by cutting with the sharp ax of knowledge and detachment the thick and impenetrable interlacing of the choking materialistic and impersonal philosophies that were stealing from us the light and our very same breath. And you were always there, ready to protect us from the attacks of the witch Maya, from the blinding whirls of material desires, from the shallow rivers of dry speculative theories and from the devouring mouth of the impersonal *mukti,* and always ready to guide us, with unflinching determination, careless of compromises and dualities, toward one and only one direction–out from this material desert, toward the eternal realm of Goloka Vṛndāvana.

As a perfect father, you have brought us up under your expert care with great love, always patiently bearing our unjustifiable offenses and untiringly correcting our repeated mistakes. And at the end you left us with a priceless inheritance to enjoy forever: the knowledge that perhaps not even Brahma can approach.

Sanātana Gosvāmī told Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: "The conclusions that You have told me are the ocean of the ambrosia of truth. My mind is unable to approach even a drop of that ocean. If You want to make a lame man like me dance, kindly bestow Your transcendental blessings by keeping Your lotus feet on my head. Now, will You please tell me, 'Let whatever I have instructed all be fully manifest unto you.' By benedicting me in this way, You will give me strength to describe all this." Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu then placed His hand on Sanātana Gosvāmī's head and benedicted him, saying, "Let all these instructions be manifest to you."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are even more merciful than the Supreme Lord because you have benedicted us, who were the lowest of mankind, with the power to spread the holy name and preach your message. Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī writes in his *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's mercy cannot be described expansively; how can we, therefore, describe your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, which is even greater? If a person just hears about your glories, very soon he will come to realize life's ultimate goal–love of Kṛṣṇa.

## Kathmandu

*Our dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,*Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. It is stated in *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta (Antya-līlā,* 2.3-4),

*sarva-loka uddhārite gaura-avatāra*  
*nistarera hetu tāra tri-vidha prakāra*

*sākṣāt-darśana, āra yogya-bhakta-jīve*  
*'āveśa' karaye kāhāṅ, kāhāṅ 'āvirbhāve'*

**"In His incarnation as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa descended to deliver all the living beings in the three worlds, from Brahmaloka down to Pātālaloka. He caused their deliverance in three ways. The Lord delivered the fallen souls in some places by meeting them directly, in other places by empowering a pure devotee, and in still other places by appearing before someone Himself."**

It is further stated,

*tāsabā tārite prabhu sei saba deśe*  
*yogya-bhakta jīva-dehe karena ‘āveśe’*

*sei jīve nija-bhakti karena prakāśe*  
*tāhāra darśane ‘vaiṣṇava’ haya sarva-deśe*

**"To deliver people in regions throughout the universe who could not meet Him, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally entered the bodies of pure devotees. Thus He empowered living beings (His pure devotees) by manifesting in them so much of His own devotion that people in all other countries became devotees by seeing them." (*****Antya-līlā,*** **2.13-14)**

Therefore we can understand that you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are such a personality. You are the embodiment of Śrī Caitanya Himself, the most merciful incarnation. Unless one is empowered by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, one cannot possibly spread the holy names of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* throughout the world, as Your Divine Grace did. Your exemplary behavior was a lesson for the whole world.

Just as Śrī Caitanya delivered the most fallen conditioned living entities like Jagāi and Mādhāi, you also possessed the same all-compassionate nature and thus saved us from a fate of suffering and punishment inconceivable to our tiny minds. One time in London you said, "Man is the architect of his own fortune, so make your fortune now. We don't mind what is your background. Whatever is done is done. Now begin, now start a new chapter in your life and in the next life go back home, back to Godhead." You made it clear that no one is disqualified from performing devotional service if he is ready to receive the mercy of the spiritual master. *Ācāryera mata yei, sei mata sāra':* "The order of the spiritual master is the active principle in spiritual life...." (*Cc. Ādi* 12.l0)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal father and we your eternal sons. The son is considered an expansion of the father; therefore the teachings of the son are nondifferent from the father's. Similarly, your instructions are nondifferent from Your Divine Grace. This is all proven by the fact that we are still able to remain Kṛṣṇa conscious in spite of your physical absence.

Śrīla Haṁsadūta Swami once confided in us that one night he had a dream he visited you in Vṛndāvana, and when you were told of this you replied, "Yes, he had come." So we can understand that for the sincere devotee you are always by his side. In his waking and sleeping hours you are always there, protecting, advising, guiding him over the ocean of nescience. We should never doubt your presence for a second; you are watching our every step, witnessing both success and failure. How can it be possible to ever devīate from your lotus feet if we are always mindful of your divine presence?

O divine master, the extent of your mercy knows no bounds. We are forever anxious to remain in the shade of your lotus feet, for in this world you are our only solace. As you recorded in your diary on your historic sea voyage to America in 1965, "I have left Bhārata-bhūmi just to execute the order of Śrī Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī in pursuance of Lord Caitanya's order. I have no qualification, but I have taken up this path to carry out the order of His Divine Grace. I depend fully on their mercy so far away from Vṛndāvana." (Sept. 10, 1965)

Ever dependent on your mercy, we beg to remain your most insignificant servants.

*The devotees of Nepal Yatra,*
